import { createCustomElement } from '@angular/elements';
import { createApplication } from '@angular/platform-browser';
import { MyTasksComponent } from './my-tasks/my-tasks.component';

async function registerMyTasksElement(): Promise<void> {
  const application = await createApplication({ providers: [] });
  const customElement = createCustomElement(MyTasksComponent, {
    injector: application.injector,
  });

  if (!customElements.get('my-tasks-angular')) {
    customElements.define('my-tasks-angular', customElement);
  }
}

void registerMyTasksElement();

<main class="tasks-page">
  <header class="page-header">
    <div>
      <h1>My Tasks</h1>
      <p>View and manage the tasks assigned to you.</p>
    </div>
  </header>

  @if (error()) {
    <div class="feedback error" role="alert">{{ error() }}</div>
  }
  @if (notice()) {
    <div class="feedback success" role="status">{{ notice() }}</div>
  }

  @if (loading()) {
    <section class="empty-card" role="status">Loading your tasks...</section>
  } @else if (tasks().length === 0) {
    <section class="empty-card">
      <h2>No tasks assigned</h2>
      <p>You currently don't have any tasks assigned to you.</p>
    </section>
  } @else {
    <section class="table-card" aria-label="Assigned tasks">
      <div class="table-scroll">
        <table class="table-grid">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th scope="col">Event</th>
              <th scope="col">Task</th>
              <th scope="col">Deadline</th>
              <th scope="col">Status</th>
              <th scope="col">Actions</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            @for (task of tasks(); track task.assignmentId) {
              <tr [class]="'task-row overdue-' + overdueLevel(task)">
                <td class="event-name">{{ task.eventName }}</td>
                <td>{{ task.task }}</td>
                <td>{{ formatDeadline(task.deadline) }}</td>
                <td>
                  <span [class]="statusClass(task)">
                    {{ displayStatus(task) }}
                  </span>
                </td>
                <td class="actions">
                  @if (!isEventOccurredIncomplete(task)) {
                    @if (normalizedStatus(task.status).toLowerCase() === 'completed') {
                      <span class="completed-label">Completed</span>
                    } @else if (normalizedStatus(task.status).toLowerCase() === 'declined') {
                      <span class="declined-label">Declined</span>
                    } @else {
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        class="complete-button"
                        [disabled]="savingAssignmentId() === task.assignmentId"
                        (click)="markAsCompleted(task)"
                      >
                        {{ savingAssignmentId() === task.assignmentId ? 'Saving...' : 'Mark Complete' }}
                      </button>
                      @if (normalizedStatus(task.status).toLowerCase() === 'pending') {
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          class="decline-button"
                          [disabled]="savingAssignmentId() === task.assignmentId"
                          (click)="confirmDecline(task)"
                        >
                          Opt Out
                        </button>
                      }
                    }
                  }
                </td>
              </tr>
            }
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </section>
  }

  @if (taskToDecline(); as selectedTask) {
    <div class="modal-backdrop">
      <section class="confirm-modal" role="alertdialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="decline-title">
        <button type="button" class="modal-close-button" aria-label="Close opt out confirmation" (click)="cancelDecline()">&times;</button>
        <h2 id="decline-title">Opt out of this task?</h2>
        <p>
          You’ll be removed from <strong>{{ selectedTask.task }}</strong> for
          <strong>{{ selectedTask.eventName }}</strong>.
        </p>
        <div class="modal-actions">
          <button type="button" class="cancel-button" (click)="cancelDecline()">Keep Task</button>
          <button
            type="button"
            class="decline-confirm-button"
            [disabled]="savingAssignmentId() === selectedTask.assignmentId"
            (click)="declineTask()"
          >
            {{ savingAssignmentId() === selectedTask.assignmentId ? 'Opting out...' : 'Confirm Opt Out' }}
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  }
</main>


================================================================================
FILE: angular-tasks/src/my-tasks/my-tasks.component.ts
================================================================================
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { Component, Input, signal } from '@angular/core';
import type { OnDestroy, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import {
  formatTaskDeadline,
  isEventOccurredIncomplete as checkEventOccurredIncomplete,
  isFinalTaskStatus,
  isTaskOverdue,
  normalizedTaskStatus,
  taskOverdueLevel,
} from './task-helpers';

interface EmployeeTask {
  assignmentId: number;
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
  eventEndDate: string | null;
  employeeId: number;
  name: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  task: string;
  deadline: string | null;
  status: string | number;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-my-tasks-angular',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule],
  templateUrl: './my-tasks.component.html',
  styleUrl: './my-tasks.component.css',
})
export class MyTasksComponent implements OnInit, OnDestroy {
  @Input() tokenProvider: (() => Promise<string>) | null = null;
  @Input() apiBaseUrl = '';

  readonly tasks = signal<EmployeeTask[]>([]);
  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly error = signal('');
  readonly notice = signal('');
  readonly currentTime = signal(Date.now());
  readonly savingAssignmentId = signal<number | null>(null);
  readonly taskToDecline = signal<EmployeeTask | null>(null);

  private timerId: number | undefined;

  async ngOnInit(): Promise<void> {
    this.timerId = window.setInterval(() => this.currentTime.set(Date.now()), 15_000);
    await this.loadTasks();
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.timerId !== undefined) {
      window.clearInterval(this.timerId);
    }
  }

  async loadTasks(): Promise<void> {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.error.set('');

    try {
      const [taskResponse, eventResponse] = await Promise.all([
        this.request('/api/employees/my-tasks'),
        this.request('/api/events'),
      ]);
      if (!taskResponse.ok || !eventResponse.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to load tasks or event dates. Status: ${taskResponse.status}/${eventResponse.status}`);
      }

      const [tasks, events] = await Promise.all([
        taskResponse.json() as Promise<EmployeeTask[]>,
        eventResponse.json() as Promise<Array<{ eventId: number; endDate: string }>>,
      ]);
      const eventEndDates = new Map(events.map((event) => [event.eventId, event.endDate]));
      this.tasks.set(
        tasks.map((task) => ({ ...task, eventEndDate: eventEndDates.get(task.eventId) ?? null })),
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load employee tasks:', error);
      this.error.set('Unable to load your assigned tasks. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      this.loading.set(false);
    }
  }

  async markAsCompleted(task: EmployeeTask): Promise<void> {
    if (this.isEventOccurredIncomplete(task)) return;
    this.savingAssignmentId.set(task.assignmentId);
    this.error.set('');
    this.notice.set('');

    try {
      const response = await this.request(`/api/staff/${task.assignmentId}`, {
        method: 'PUT',
        body: JSON.stringify({ task: task.task, deadline: task.deadline, status: 2 }),
      });
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to complete task. Status: ${response.status}`);
      }

      this.replaceTask(task.assignmentId, { ...task, status: 'Completed' });
      this.notice.set(`Task "${task.task}" is marked complete.`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to mark task as completed:', error);
      this.error.set('Unable to mark the task as completed. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      this.savingAssignmentId.set(null);
    }
  }

  confirmDecline(task: EmployeeTask): void {
    if (this.isEventOccurredIncomplete(task)) return;
    this.taskToDecline.set(task);
    this.error.set('');
    this.notice.set('');
  }

  cancelDecline(): void {
    this.taskToDecline.set(null);
  }

  async declineTask(): Promise<void> {
    const task = this.taskToDecline();
    if (!task) return;
    if (this.isEventOccurredIncomplete(task)) {
      this.taskToDecline.set(null);
      return;
    }

    this.savingAssignmentId.set(task.assignmentId);
    this.error.set('');
    this.notice.set('');

    try {
      const response = await this.request(`/api/staff/${task.assignmentId}`, {
        method: 'PUT',
        body: JSON.stringify({ task: task.task, deadline: task.deadline, status: 3 }),
      });
      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();
        throw new Error(message || `Failed to opt out. Status: ${response.status}`);
      }

      this.replaceTask(task.assignmentId, { ...task, status: 'Declined' });
      this.taskToDecline.set(null);
      this.notice.set(`Task "${task.task}" has been declined.`);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to decline employee task:', error);
      this.error.set(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unable to opt out of this task.');
    } finally {
      this.savingAssignmentId.set(null);
    }
  }

  isOverdue(task: EmployeeTask): boolean {
    return isTaskOverdue(task.deadline, task.status, this.currentTime());
  }

  overdueLevel(task: EmployeeTask): number {
    return taskOverdueLevel(task, this.currentTime());
  }

  formatDeadline(deadline: string | null): string {
    return formatTaskDeadline(deadline);
  }

  isEventOccurredIncomplete(task: EmployeeTask): boolean {
    return checkEventOccurredIncomplete(task, this.currentTime());
  }

  normalizedStatus(status: string | number): string {
    return normalizedTaskStatus(status);
  }

  displayStatus(task: EmployeeTask): string {
    return this.isEventOccurredIncomplete(task)
      ? 'Event Occurred, Task Incomplete'
      : this.normalizedStatus(task.status);
  }

  statusClass(task: EmployeeTask): string {
    return this.isEventOccurredIncomplete(task)
      ? 'status event-incomplete'
      : `status ${this.normalizedStatus(task.status).toLowerCase().replaceAll(' ', '-')}`;
  }

  isFinalStatus(status: string | number): boolean {
    return isFinalTaskStatus(status);
  }
  private async request(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<Response> {
    if (!this.tokenProvider) {
      throw new Error('Authentication is not ready. Please refresh the page.');
    }

    const accessToken = await this.tokenProvider();
    const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
    headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${accessToken}`);
    if (init.body) headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');

    return fetch(`${this.apiBaseUrl}${path}`, { ...init, headers });
  }

  private replaceTask(assignmentId: number, updatedTask: EmployeeTask): void {
    this.tasks.update((current) =>
      current.map((task) => (task.assignmentId === assignmentId ? updatedTask : task)),
    );
  }
}


================================================================================
FILE: angular-tasks/src/my-tasks/task-helpers.ts
================================================================================
export interface TaskStatusInfo {
  status: string | number;
  deadline: string | null;
  eventEndDate: string | null;
}

export function normalizedTaskStatus(status: string | number): string {
  const value = String(status).toLowerCase();
  const labels: Record<string, string> = {
    '0': 'Pending',
    '1': 'In Progress',
    '2': 'Completed',
    '3': 'Declined',
  };
  return labels[value] ?? String(status);
}

export function isFinalTaskStatus(status: string | number): boolean {
  const normalized = normalizedTaskStatus(status).toLowerCase();
  return normalized === 'completed' || normalized === 'declined';
}

export function isTaskOverdue(deadline: string | null, status: string | number, currentTime: number): boolean {
  if (!deadline || isFinalTaskStatus(status)) return false;
  const deadlineTime = new Date(deadline).getTime();
  return Number.isFinite(deadlineTime) && currentTime > deadlineTime;
}

export function taskOverdueLevel(task: TaskStatusInfo, currentTime: number): number {
  if (!isTaskOverdue(task.deadline, task.status, currentTime) || !task.deadline) return 0;
  const elapsed = currentTime - new Date(task.deadline).getTime();
  return Math.min(4, Math.floor(elapsed / (90 * 60 * 1000)) + 1);
}

export function formatTaskDeadline(deadline: string | null): string {
  if (!deadline) return 'No deadline';
  const date = new Date(deadline);
  if (!Number.isFinite(date.getTime())) return 'Invalid deadline';
  return date.toLocaleString([], {
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    hour: 'numeric',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });
}

export function isEventOccurredIncomplete(task: TaskStatusInfo, currentTime: number): boolean {
  if (!task.eventEndDate || !isTaskOverdue(task.deadline, task.status, currentTime)) return false;
  const eventEnd = new Date(task.eventEndDate).getTime();
  return Number.isFinite(eventEnd) && eventEnd < currentTime;
}

================================================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================================================
# WinEvents Frontend

React, TypeScript, Vite, and Tailwind CSS frontend for the WinEvents event management application.

## Requirements

- Node.js 22 (see `.nvmrc`)
- npm

## Setup and commands

Run these commands from the `Frontend` directory:

```powershell
npm install
npm run dev
```

Project checks:

```powershell
npm test
npm run test:coverage
npm run lint
npm run typecheck
npm run format:check
npm run build
```

Vitest and V8 collect coverage. The minimum is 80% for both line and branch coverage.

## Configuration

The development server proxies `/api` to `http://localhost:5244`. Set `VITE_API_BASE_URL` in a local `.env` file only when the frontend should call a different API origin. `.env.example` documents the optional setting. Do not commit secrets.

## Source layout

- `src/Pages`: route-level screens
- `src/Components`: reusable UI components
- `src/auth`: Microsoft authentication setup
- `src/config`: validated environment configuration
- `src/shared/api`: shared API request helper
- `tests`: Vitest and React Testing Library unit tests, MSW setup, and test utilities

TypeScript is strict. Use the shared API client, reusable components, semantic HTML, and Tailwind utility classes. Keep tests in `tests` rather than beside production components.


================================================================================
FILE: src/app/App.tsx
================================================================================
import { Suspense, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import {
  createBrowserRouter,
  Navigate,
  Link,
  Outlet,
  RouterProvider,
  useRouteError,
  type RouteObject,
} from 'react-router-dom';

import { useMsal } from '@azure/msal-react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

import Login from '../shared/components/Login';
import Navigationbar from '../shared/components/Navigationbar';
import { AppProviders } from './providers/AppProviders';
import '../i18n';

function RouteLoading() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-64 items-center justify-center" role="status">
      <span>{t('loadingPage')}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

function RouteError() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const error = useRouteError();
  console.error('Route failed to load:', error);

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto max-w-2xl p-8" role="alert">
      <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">{t('routeErrorTitle')}</h1>
      <p className="mt-2">{t('routeErrorMessage')}</p>
    </section>
  );
}

function NotFound() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <section className="mx-auto max-w-2xl p-8">
      <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">{t('notFoundTitle')}</h1>
      <p className="mt-2">{t('notFoundMessage')}</p>
      <Link className="mt-4 inline-block underline" to="/dashboard">
        {t('returnToDashboard')}
      </Link>
    </section>
  );
}

function MainLayout() {
  return (
    <div className="page-canvas min-h-screen">
      <Navigationbar />

      <main className="min-h-[calc(100vh-73px)]">
        <Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}>
          <Outlet />
        </Suspense>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

//Protect pages by Entra roles.
function RoleRoute({ allowedRoles, children }: { allowedRoles: string[]; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { instance, accounts } = useMsal();

  const account = instance.getActiveAccount() ?? accounts[0];

  const roles = (account?.idTokenClaims?.roles as string[]) ?? [];

  const hasPermission = allowedRoles.some((role) => roles.includes(role));

  if (!hasPermission) {
    return <Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />;
  }

  return children;
}

function createRoutes(): RouteObject[] {
  return [
    {
      path: '/',
      element: <Login />,
      errorElement: <RouteError />,
    },
    {
      element: <MainLayout />,
      errorElement: <RouteError />,
      HydrateFallback: RouteLoading,
      children: [
        {
          path: '/dashboard',
          lazy: async () => ({ Component: (await import('../features/dashboard')).default }),
        },
        {
          path: '/events',
          lazy: async () => ({ Component: (await import('../features/events')).default }),
        },
        {
          path: '/events/:eventId',
          lazy: async () => ({ Component: (await import('../features/events')).default }),
        },
        {
          path: '/participants',
          element: (
            <RoleRoute allowedRoles={['Admin', 'Employee']}>
              <Outlet />
            </RoleRoute>
          ),
          children: [
            {
              index: true,
              lazy: async () => ({ Component: (await import('../features/participants')).default }),
            },
          ],
        },
        {
          path: '/my-tasks',
          element: (
            <RoleRoute allowedRoles={['Admin', 'Employee']}>
              <Outlet />
            </RoleRoute>
          ),
          children: [
            {
              index: true,
              lazy: async () => ({
                Component: (await import('../features/tasks/pages/MyTaskPageAngular')).default,
              }),
            },
          ],
        },
        {
          path: '/administration',
          element: (
            <RoleRoute allowedRoles={['Admin']}>
              <Outlet />
            </RoleRoute>
          ),
          children: [
            {
              index: true,
              lazy: async () => ({
                Component: (await import('../features/administration')).default,
              }),
            },
          ],
        },
        { path: '*', element: <NotFound /> },
      ],
    },
  ];
}

export function App() {
  const router = useMemo(() => createBrowserRouter(createRoutes()), []);
  return (
    <AppProviders>
      <RouterProvider router={router} />
    </AppProviders>
  );
}

import { StrictMode } from 'react';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import { MsalProvider } from '@azure/msal-react';

import '../index.css';
import '@syncfusion/ej2-tailwind3-theme/styles/schedule/index.css';

import { App } from './App';
import { msalInstance } from '../auth/msalInstance';
import { i18nReady } from '../i18n';

const rootElement = document.getElementById('root');

if (!rootElement) {
  throw new Error('Root element not found');
}

// Create the React application root
const root = createRoot(rootElement);

async function startApp() {
  // Initialize MSAL before rendering the application
  await Promise.all([msalInstance.initialize(), i18nReady]);

  root.render(
    <StrictMode>
      <MsalProvider instance={msalInstance}>
        <App />
      </MsalProvider>
    </StrictMode>,
  );
}

startApp().catch((error) => {
  console.error('Failed to start application:', error);
});

    clientId: '0332cc25-1dc3-4542-b1cd-a1ad23d0f620',
    authority: 'https://login.microsoftonline.com/bdcfaa46-3f69-4dfd-b3f7-c582bdfbb820',
    redirectUri: 'http://localhost:5173/',
  },

  cache: {
    cacheLocation: 'sessionStorage',
  },

  system: {
    loggerOptions: {
      loggerCallback: (level, message, containsPii) => {
        if (containsPii) {
          return;
        }

        switch (level) {
          case LogLevel.Error:
            console.error(message);
            break;

          case LogLevel.Warning:
            console.warn(message);
            break;

          case LogLevel.Info:
            break;
        }
      },
    },
  },
};

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

interface AdminDataTableProps {
  headers: string[];
  minWidth: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}

function AdminDataTable({ headers, minWidth, children }: AdminDataTableProps) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-border-subtle">
      <table className="table-grid w-full border-collapse text-left" style={{ minWidth }}>
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-border-subtle bg-surface-muted">
            {headers.map((header) => (
              <th
                key={header}
                className="px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-text-muted"
              >
                {header}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>{children}</tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

export default AdminDataTable;

import type { ChangeEvent, FormEvent } from 'react';

import EmptyTableRow from '@/shared/components/EmptyTableRow';
import FormField from '@/shared/components/FormField';
import Modal from '@/shared/components/Modal';

export interface Organization {
  orgId: number;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  contactPerson: string;
}

export interface Employee {
  employeeId: number;

  jobTitle: string;
  email: string;
  orgId: number;
}

export interface Venue {
  venueId: number;
  address: string;
  capacity: number;

export interface OrganizationFormData {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  contactPerson: string;

  name: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  email: string;
  orgId: string;

  address: string;
  capacity: string;

interface OrganizationSectionProps {
  items: Organization[];
  searchValue: string;

  editingId: number | null;
  loading: boolean;
  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void;
  onOpenAdd: () => void;
  onSubmit: (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => void;
  onChange: (event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
  onEdit: (item: Organization) => void;
  onDelete: (id: number) => void;
}

export function OrganizationSection({
  items,
  searchValue,
  form,
  formOpen,
  editingId,
  loading,
  onSearchChange,
  onOpenAdd,
  onSubmit,
  onChange,
  onCancel,
  onEdit,
  onDelete,
}: OrganizationSectionProps) {
  return (

      title="Organizations"
      description="Create and manage event organizations."
      searchValue={searchValue}
      onSearchChange={onSearchChange}
      searchPlaceholder="Search organizations..."
      addLabel="+ Add Organization"
      onAdd={onOpenAdd}
    >
      {formOpen && (
        <Modal
          open={formOpen}
          ariaLabel={editingId === null ? 'Add organization' : 'Edit organization'}
          onClose={onCancel}
          className="max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-2xl overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-border-accent/40 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl"
        >
        <form onSubmit={onSubmit}>
          <h3 className="mb-5 text-base font-semibold text-text-primary">
            {editingId === null ? 'Add Organization' : 'Edit Organization'}
          </h3>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            <FormField label="Name" required>
              <input
                type="text"
                name="name"
                value={form.name}
                onChange={onChange}
                required
                className={inputClass}
              />
            </FormField>
            <FormField label="Email" required>
              <input
                type="email"
                name="email"
                value={form.email}
                onChange={onChange}
                required
                className={inputClass}
              />
            </FormField>
            <FormField label="Phone" required>
              <input
                type="text"
                name="phone"
                value={form.phone}
                onChange={onChange}
                required
                className={inputClass}
              />
            </FormField>
            <FormField label="Contact Person">
              <input
                type="text"
                name="contactPerson"
                value={form.contactPerson}
                onChange={onChange}
                className={inputClass}
              />
            </FormField>
          </div>
          <FormActions
            loading={loading}
            editing={editingId !== null}
            addLabel="Add Organization"
            updateLabel="Update Organization"
          />
        </form>
        </Modal>
      )}

      <AdminDataTable
        minWidth="760px"
        headers={['Organization', 'Email', 'Phone', 'Contact Person', 'Actions']}
      >
        {items.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyTableRow
            colSpan={5}
            title={searchValue ? 'No matching organizations' : 'No organizations found'}
            description={
              searchValue ? 'Try a different search.' : 'Organizations will appear here.'
            }
          />
        ) : (
          items.map((item) => (
            <tr key={item.orgId} className={rowClass}>
              <td className={cellClass}>{item.name}</td>
              <td className={cellClass}>{item.email}</td>
              <td className={cellClass}>{item.phone}</td>
              <td className={cellClass}>{item.contactPerson || '-'}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                <RowActions onEdit={() => onEdit(item)} onDelete={() => onDelete(item.orgId)} />
              </td>

    </AdminSectionPanel>

}

              loading={loading}
              editing={isEditing}
              addLabel="Add Venue"
              updateLabel="Update Venue"
            />
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}

      <AdminDataTable minWidth="760px" headers={['Venue', 'Location', 'Capacity', 'Actions']}>
        {items.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyTableRow
            colSpan={4}

            description={searchValue ? 'Try a different search.' : 'Venues will appear here.'}
          />
        ) : (
          items.map((item) => (
            <tr key={item.venueId} className={rowClass}>
              <td className={cellClass}>{item.address}</td>
              <td className={cellClass}>{item.address}</td>

              </td>

  );
}
const inputClass =
  'w-full rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-text-primary outline-none transition placeholder:text-text-placeholder focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-primary/20';
const rowClass = 'border-b border-border-subtle last:border-b-0 hover:bg-surface-muted';
const cellClass = 'px-4 py-3 text-sm text-text-secondary';

interface FormActionsProps {
  loading: boolean;
  editing: boolean;
  addLabel: string;
  updateLabel: string;
}

function FormActions({ loading, editing, addLabel, updateLabel }: FormActionsProps) {
  return (
    <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-3">
      <button
        type="submit"
        className="rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
        disabled={loading}
      >
        {loading ? 'Saving...' : editing ? updateLabel : addLabel}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

interface RowActionsProps {
  onEdit: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
}

function RowActions({ onEdit, onDelete }: RowActionsProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
      <button
        type="button"
        className="rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary transition hover:border-brand-primary hover:bg-surface-blue hover:text-brand-primary-hover"
        onClick={onEdit}
      >
        Edit
      </button>
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-label="Delete"
        title="Delete"
        className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-lg border border-surface-error bg-white p-0 text-state-error transition hover:bg-surface-error"
        onClick={onDelete}
      >
        <svg
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="h-4 w-4"
          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          fill="none"
          stroke="currentColor"
          strokeWidth="1.8"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeLinejoin="round"
        >
          <path d="M3 6h18M8 6V4h8v2m2 0-1 14H7L6 6m4 4v6m4-6v6" />
        </svg>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import SearchBar from '@/shared/components/SearchBar';

interface AdminSectionPanelProps {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  searchValue: string;
  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void;
  searchPlaceholder: string;
  addLabel: string;
  onAdd: () => void;
  children: ReactNode;
}

function AdminSectionPanel({
  title,
  description,
  searchValue,
  onSearchChange,
  searchPlaceholder,
  addLabel,
  onAdd,
  children,
}: AdminSectionPanelProps) {
  return (
    <section className="glass-panel p-6">
      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-end">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-text-primary">{title}</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-muted">{description}</p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row md:w-auto">
          <SearchBar
            value={searchValue}
            onChange={onSearchChange}
            placeholder={searchPlaceholder}
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            className="whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
            onClick={onAdd}
          >
            {addLabel}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {children}
    </section>
  );
}

export default AdminSectionPanel;

================================================================================
FILE: src/features/administration/pages/AdministrationPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { apiRequest } from '../../../shared/api/apiClient';
import { useAccessToken } from '@/shared/hooks/useAccessToken';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import PageHeader from '@/shared/components/PageHeader';
import {
  EmployeeSection,
  OrganizationSection,
  VenueSection,
} from '@/features/administration/components/AdministrationSections';

type AdminSection = 'organizations' | 'employees' | 'venues';

interface Organization {
  orgId: number;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  contactPerson: string;
}

interface Employee {
  employeeId: number;
  name: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  email: string;
  orgId: number;
}

interface Venue {
  venueId: number;
  address: string;
  capacity: number;
}

interface OrganizationForm {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  contactPerson: string;
}

interface EmployeeForm {
  name: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  email: string;
  orgId: string;
}

interface VenueForm {
  address: string;
  capacity: string;
}

function AdministrationPage() {
  const { getAccessToken } = useAccessToken();

  const [activeSection, setActiveSection] = useState<AdminSection>('organizations');

  const [organizations, setOrganizations] = useState<Organization[]>([]);

  const [employees, setEmployees] = useState<Employee[]>([]);

  const [venues, setVenues] = useState<Venue[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  // FILTERS

  const [organizationSearch, setOrganizationSearch] = useState('');

  const [employeeSearch, setEmployeeSearch] = useState('');

  const [venueSearch, setVenueSearch] = useState('');

  // Organization form

  const [showOrganizationForm, setShowOrganizationForm] = useState(false);

  const [editingOrganizationId, setEditingOrganizationId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [organizationForm, setOrganizationForm] = useState<OrganizationForm>({
    name: '',
    email: '',
    phone: '',
    contactPerson: '',
  });

  // Employee form

  const [showEmployeeForm, setShowEmployeeForm] = useState(false);

  const [editingEmployeeId, setEditingEmployeeId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [employeeForm, setEmployeeForm] = useState<EmployeeForm>({
    name: '',
    jobTitle: '',
    email: '',
    orgId: '',
  });

  // Venue form

  const [showVenueForm, setShowVenueForm] = useState(false);

  const [editingVenueId, setEditingVenueId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [venueForm, setVenueForm] = useState<VenueForm>({
    address: '',
    capacity: '',
  });

  // AUTH HEADERS

  const getAuthHeaders = async (): Promise<HeadersInit> => {
    const token = await getAccessToken();
    return { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token };
  };

  // LOAD ORGANIZATIONS

  const loadOrganizations = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');

      const response = await apiRequest('/api/organizations', {
        method: 'GET',
        headers: {
          ...(await getAuthHeaders()),
        },
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to load organizations (${response.status})`);
      }

      const data = await response.json();

      setOrganizations(data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to load organizations.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // LOAD EMPLOYEES

  const loadEmployees = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');

      const response = await apiRequest('/api/employees', {
        method: 'GET',
        headers: {
          ...(await getAuthHeaders()),
        },
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to load employees (${response.status})`);
      }

      const data = await response.json();

      setEmployees(data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to load employees.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // LOAD VENUES

  const loadVenues = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');

      const response = await apiRequest('/api/venues', {
        method: 'GET',
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to load venues (${response.status})`);
      }

      const data = await response.json();

      setVenues(data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to load venues.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // LOAD DATA WHEN TAB CHANGES

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeSection === 'organizations') {
      loadOrganizations();
    }

    if (activeSection === 'employees') {
      loadOrganizations();
      loadEmployees();
    }

    if (activeSection === 'venues') {
      loadVenues();
    }
    // The data loaders intentionally run only when the selected section changes.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [activeSection]);

  // ORGANIZATION FORM

  const openAddOrganization = () => {
    setEditingOrganizationId(null);

    setOrganizationForm({
      name: '',
      email: '',
      phone: '',
      contactPerson: '',
    });

    setShowOrganizationForm(true);
    setError('');
  };

  const openEditOrganization = (organization: Organization) => {
    setEditingOrganizationId(organization.orgId);

    setOrganizationForm({
      name: organization.name,
      email: organization.email,
      phone: organization.phone,
      contactPerson: organization.contactPerson || '',
    });

    setShowOrganizationForm(true);
    setError('');
  };

  const handleOrganizationChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;

    setOrganizationForm((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  };

  const saveOrganization = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');

      const isEditing = editingOrganizationId !== null;

      const url = isEditing ? `/api/organizations/${editingOrganizationId}` : '/api/organizations';

      const response = await apiRequest(url, {
        method: isEditing ? 'PUT' : 'POST',

        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          ...(await getAuthHeaders()),
        },

        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: organizationForm.name,

          email: organizationForm.email,

          phone: organizationForm.phone,

          contactPerson: organizationForm.contactPerson || null,
        }),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();

        throw new Error(
          message ||
            `Failed to ${isEditing ? 'update' : 'create'} organization (${response.status})`,
        );
      }

      setShowOrganizationForm(false);

      setEditingOrganizationId(null);

      await loadOrganizations();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to save organization.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // DELETE ORGANIZATION

  const deleteOrganization = async (organizationId: number) => {
    const confirmed = window.confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this organization?');

    if (!confirmed) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');

      const response = await apiRequest(`/api/organizations/${organizationId}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: {
          ...(await getAuthHeaders()),
        },
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();

        throw new Error(message || `Failed to delete organization (${response.status})`);
      }

      await loadOrganizations();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to delete organization.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // EMPLOYEE FORM

  const openAddEmployee = () => {
    setEditingEmployeeId(null);

    setEmployeeForm({
      name: '',
      jobTitle: '',
      email: '',
      orgId: '',
    });

    setShowEmployeeForm(true);
    setError('');
  };

  const openEditEmployee = (employee: Employee) => {
    setEditingEmployeeId(employee.employeeId);

    setEmployeeForm({
      name: employee.name,
      jobTitle: employee.jobTitle || '',
      email: employee.email,
      orgId: employee.orgId.toString(),
    });

    setShowEmployeeForm(true);
    setError('');
  };

  const handleEmployeeChange = (
    event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement> | React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>,
  ) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;

    setEmployeeForm((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  };

  const saveEmployee = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');

      const isEditing = editingEmployeeId !== null;

      const url = isEditing ? `/api/employees/${editingEmployeeId}` : '/api/employees';

      const response = await apiRequest(url, {
        method: isEditing ? 'PUT' : 'POST',

        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          ...(await getAuthHeaders()),
        },

        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: employeeForm.name,

          jobTitle: employeeForm.jobTitle,

          email: employeeForm.email,

          orgId: Number(employeeForm.orgId),
        }),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();

        throw new Error(
          message || `Failed to ${isEditing ? 'update' : 'create'} employee (${response.status})`,
        );
      }

      setShowEmployeeForm(false);

      setEditingEmployeeId(null);

      await loadEmployees();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to save employee.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // DELETE EMPLOYEE

  const deleteEmployee = async (employeeId: number) => {
    const confirmed = window.confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this employee?');

    if (!confirmed) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');

      const response = await apiRequest(`/api/employees/${employeeId}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: {
          ...(await getAuthHeaders()),
        },
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();

        throw new Error(message || `Failed to delete employee (${response.status})`);
      }

      await loadEmployees();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to delete employee.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // VENUE FORM

  const openAddVenue = () => {
    setEditingVenueId(null);
    setVenueForm({
      address: '',
      capacity: '',
    });

    setShowVenueForm(true);
    setError('');
  };

  const openEditVenue = (venue: Venue) => {
    setEditingVenueId(venue.venueId);
    setVenueForm({
      address: venue.address,
      capacity: String(venue.capacity),
    });

    setShowVenueForm(true);
    setError('');
  };

  const closeVenueForm = () => {
    setShowVenueForm(false);
    setEditingVenueId(null);
  };

  const handleVenueChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;

    setVenueForm((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  };

  const saveVenue = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();

    const isEditing = editingVenueId !== null;
    const url = isEditing ? `/api/venues/${editingVenueId}` : '/api/venues';

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');

      const response = await apiRequest(url, {
        method: isEditing ? 'PUT' : 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          ...(await getAuthHeaders()),
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          address: venueForm.address,
          capacity: Number(venueForm.capacity),
        }),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();
        throw new Error(message || `Failed to ${isEditing ? 'update' : 'create'} venue (${response.status})`);
      }

      closeVenueForm();
      await loadVenues();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to save venue.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const deleteVenue = async (venueId: number) => {
    const confirmed = window.confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this venue?');
    if (!confirmed) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');

      const response = await apiRequest(`/api/venues/${venueId}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: {
          ...(await getAuthHeaders()),
        },
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();
        throw new Error(message || `Failed to delete venue (${response.status})`);
      }

      await loadVenues();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to delete venue.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };
  // ORGANIZATION NAME

  const getOrganizationName = (orgId: number) => {
    const organization = organizations.find((item) => item.orgId === orgId);

    return organization ? organization.name : 'Unknown';
  };

  // FILTERED DATA

  const filteredOrganizations = organizations.filter((organization) => {
    const search = organizationSearch.toLowerCase().trim();

    if (!search) {
      return true;
    }

    return (
      organization.name.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
      organization.email.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
      organization.phone.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
      organization.contactPerson.toLowerCase().includes(search)
    );
  });

  const filteredEmployees = employees.filter((employee) => {
    const search = employeeSearch.toLowerCase().trim();

    if (!search) {
      return true;
    }

    const organizationName = getOrganizationName(employee.orgId);

    return (
      employee.name.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
      employee.email.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
      employee.jobTitle.toLowerCase().includes(search) ||
      organizationName.toLowerCase().includes(search)
    );
  });

  const filteredVenues = venues.filter((venue) => {
    const search = venueSearch.toLowerCase().trim();

    if (!search) {
      return true;
    }

    return (
      venue.address.toLowerCase().includes(search) || venue.capacity.toString().includes(search)
    );
  });

  // UI

  return (
    <div className="page-canvas min-h-full p-6 md:p-8">
      <PageHeader
        title="Administration"
        description="Manage organizations, employees, and venues."
        layout="responsive"
      />

      {/* ERROR */}

      {error && (
        <div className="mb-6 rounded-lg border border-error-border bg-surface-error px-4 py-3 text-sm text-state-error-strong">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TABS */}

      <div className="glass-panel mb-6 flex flex-wrap gap-1 p-1">
        <button
          type="button"
          className={`rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition ${
            activeSection === 'organizations'
              ? 'bg-surface-blue text-brand-primary'
              : 'text-text-muted hover:bg-surface-subtle hover:text-text-secondary'
          }`}
          onClick={() => setActiveSection('organizations')}
        >
          Organizations
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={`rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition ${
            activeSection === 'employees'
              ? 'bg-surface-blue text-brand-primary'
              : 'text-text-muted hover:bg-surface-subtle hover:text-text-secondary'
          }`}
          onClick={() => setActiveSection('employees')}
        >
          Employees
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={`rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition ${
            activeSection === 'venues'
              ? 'bg-surface-blue text-brand-primary'
              : 'text-text-muted hover:bg-surface-subtle hover:text-text-secondary'
          }`}
          onClick={() => setActiveSection('venues')}
        >
          Venues
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ORGANIZATIONS */}
      {activeSection === 'organizations' && (
        <OrganizationSection
          items={filteredOrganizations}
          searchValue={organizationSearch}
          form={organizationForm}
          formOpen={showOrganizationForm}
          editingId={editingOrganizationId}
          loading={loading}
          onSearchChange={setOrganizationSearch}
          onOpenAdd={openAddOrganization}
          onSubmit={saveOrganization}
          onChange={handleOrganizationChange}
          onCancel={() => setShowOrganizationForm(false)}
          onEdit={openEditOrganization}
          onDelete={deleteOrganization}
        />
      )}

      {/* EMPLOYEES */}
      {activeSection === 'employees' && (
        <EmployeeSection
          items={filteredEmployees}
          organizations={organizations}
          searchValue={employeeSearch}
          form={employeeForm}
          formOpen={showEmployeeForm}
          editingId={editingEmployeeId}
          loading={loading}
          organizationName={getOrganizationName}
          onSearchChange={setEmployeeSearch}
          onOpenAdd={openAddEmployee}
          onSubmit={saveEmployee}
          onChange={handleEmployeeChange}
          onCancel={() => setShowEmployeeForm(false)}
          onEdit={openEditEmployee}
          onDelete={deleteEmployee}
        />
      )}

      {/* VENUES */}
      {activeSection === 'venues' && (
        <VenueSection
          items={filteredVenues}
          searchValue={venueSearch}
          form={venueForm}
          formOpen={showVenueForm}
          editingId={editingVenueId}
          loading={loading}
          onSearchChange={setVenueSearch}
          onOpenAdd={openAddVenue}
          onSubmit={saveVenue}
          onChange={handleVenueChange}
          onCancel={closeVenueForm}
          onEdit={openEditVenue}
          onDelete={deleteVenue}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default AdministrationPage;

FILE: src/features/dashboard/components/AttendeeEventPopup.tsx
================================================================================
interface AttendeeEvent {
  eventName: string;
  startDate: string;
  endDate: string;
  isRegistrationCancelled?: boolean;
}

interface AttendeeEventPopupProps {
  event: AttendeeEvent;
  isCompleted?: boolean;
  top: number;
  left: number;
  location: string;
  formatDate: (date: string) => string;
  formatTime: (date: string) => string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onReregister: () => void;
  reregistering: boolean;
}

function AttendeeEventPopup({
  event,
  isCompleted = false,
  top,
  left,
  location,
  formatDate,
  formatTime,
  onClose,
  onReregister,
  reregistering,
}: AttendeeEventPopupProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className="glass-popup glass-popup-orange fixed z-[1100] w-[min(20rem,calc(100vw-2rem))] p-4 text-text-orange-rich"
      style={{
        top,
        left,
      }}
      role="dialog"
      aria-labelledby="attendee-event-title"
    >
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <h3 id="attendee-event-title" className="text-base font-semibold">
          {event.eventName}
        </h3>
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Close event details"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-full text-2xl leading-none text-text-orange-deep transition hover:bg-white/60 hover:text-text-orange-rich"
        >
          &times;
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="mb-2 text-sm">
        {formatDate(event.startDate)} | {formatTime(event.startDate)} - {formatTime(event.endDate)}
      </p>
      <p className="text-sm">Location: {location}</p>
      {isCompleted && (
        <span className="mt-3 inline-flex rounded-md bg-purple-100/80 px-2.5 py-1 text-sm font-semibold text-purple-800">
          Completed
        </span>
      )}
      {event.isRegistrationCancelled && (
        <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <span className="rounded-md bg-orange-border-light px-2.5 py-1 text-sm font-semibold text-text-orange-deep">
            Cancelled
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onReregister}
            disabled={reregistering}
            className="rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            {reregistering ? 'Re-registering...' : 'Re-register'}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default AttendeeEventPopup;


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/dashboard/components/DashboardCalendarPanel.tsx
================================================================================
import {
  ScheduleComponent,
  ViewsDirective,
  ViewDirective,
  Inject,
  Month,
  Week,
  WorkWeek,
  Day,
  Agenda,
} from '@syncfusion/ej2-react-schedule';
import Modal from '@/shared/components/Modal';
import EventEditorForm from '@/features/events/components/EventEditorForm';
import type { EventFormData } from '@/features/events/schemas/eventFormSchema';
import AttendeeEventPopup from './AttendeeEventPopup';

interface CalendarEvent {
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
  startDate: string;
  endDate: string;
  eventType: number;
  status: string | number;
  venueId?: number | null;
  orgId?: number | null;
  budget?: number | null;
  isRegistrationCancelled?: boolean;
}

interface VenueOption {
  venueId: number;
  address: string;
  capacity: number;
}

interface OrganizationOption {
  orgId: number;
  name: string;
}

export interface DashboardCalendarPanelProps {
  monthEventCount: number;
  selectedDate: Date | null;
  currentDate: Date;
  schedulerEvents: Record<string, unknown>[];
  loading: boolean;
  canManageEvents: boolean;
  isAttendee: boolean;
  venues: VenueOption[];
  organizations: OrganizationOption[];
  attendeeEventPopup: {
    event: CalendarEvent;
    top: number;
    left: number;
    isCompleted?: boolean;
  } | null;
  showEventForm: boolean;
  editingEventId: number | null;
  savingEvent: boolean;
  formData: EventFormData;
  onSetCurrentDate: (date: Date) => void;
  onClearSelectedDate: () => void;
  onCreateEvent: () => void;
  onEventRendered: (args: { data: Record<string, unknown>; element: HTMLElement }) => void;
  onCellClick: (args: { startTime: Date }) => void;
  onCellDoubleClick: (args: { cancel: boolean }) => void;
  onEventDoubleClick: (args: {
    cancel: boolean;
    event?: unknown;
    data?: Record<string, unknown>;
  }) => void;
  onPopupOpen: (args: {
    type: string;
    data?: Record<string, unknown>;
    cancel: boolean;
    target?: Element;
  }) => void;
  onRenderCell: (args: { element: HTMLElement; date?: Date; type: string }) => void;
  onCloseAttendeePopup: () => void;
  onReregister: (eventId: number) => void;
  reregisteringEventId: number | null;
  formatDisplayDate: (date: string) => string;
  formatTime: (date: string) => string;
  onCloseEventForm: () => void;
  onCancelEditingEvent?: () => void;
  onSaveEvent: (values: EventFormData) => void;
}

function DashboardCalendarPanel({
  monthEventCount,
  selectedDate,
  currentDate,
  schedulerEvents,
  loading,
  canManageEvents,
  isAttendee,
  venues,
  organizations,
  attendeeEventPopup,
  showEventForm,
  editingEventId,
  savingEvent,
  formData,
  onSetCurrentDate,
  onClearSelectedDate,
  onCreateEvent,
  onEventRendered,
  onCellClick,
  onCellDoubleClick,
  onEventDoubleClick,
  onPopupOpen,
  onRenderCell,
  onCloseAttendeePopup,
  onReregister,
  reregisteringEventId,
  formatDisplayDate,
  formatTime,
  onCloseEventForm,
  onCancelEditingEvent,
  onSaveEvent,
}: DashboardCalendarPanelProps) {
  return (
    <div className="glass-panel glass-panel-blue w-full p-6">
      <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 max-[700px]:flex-col max-[700px]:items-start">
        <div>
          <h2 className="m-0 text-xl font-bold text-text-primary">Event Calendar</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-muted">
            {monthEventCount} event{monthEventCount === 1 ? '' : 's'} this month
          </p>
          {selectedDate && (
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-blue-strong">
              Selected date:{' '}
              {selectedDate.toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
                month: 'short',
                day: 'numeric',
                year: 'numeric',
              })}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        {canManageEvents && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onCreateEvent}
            className="shrink-0 rounded-lg bg-accent-orange px-4 py-2.5 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-accent-orange-hover"
          >
            + Add Event
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-lg border border-border-accent/60">
        {loading ? (
          <p className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-text-muted">Loading events...</p>
        ) : (
          <ScheduleComponent
            cssClass="dashboard-calendar"
            height="650px"
            selectedDate={currentDate}
            currentView="Month"
            readonly={!canManageEvents}
            showQuickInfo={true}
            eventSettings={{ dataSource: schedulerEvents }}
            eventRendered={onEventRendered}
            cellClick={onCellClick}
            cellDoubleClick={onCellDoubleClick}
            eventDoubleClick={onEventDoubleClick}
            popupOpen={onPopupOpen}
            renderCell={onRenderCell}
            navigating={(args) => {
              if (args.currentDate) {
                onSetCurrentDate(args.currentDate);
                onClearSelectedDate();
              }
            }}
          >
            <ViewsDirective>
              <ViewDirective option="Month" />
              <ViewDirective option="Week" />
              <ViewDirective option="WorkWeek" />
              <ViewDirective option="Day" />
              <ViewDirective option="Agenda" />
            </ViewsDirective>
            <Inject services={[Month, Week, WorkWeek, Day, Agenda]} />
          </ScheduleComponent>
        )}
      </div>

      {attendeeEventPopup && (isAttendee || attendeeEventPopup.isCompleted) && (
        <AttendeeEventPopup
          event={attendeeEventPopup.event}
          isCompleted={attendeeEventPopup.isCompleted}
          top={attendeeEventPopup.top}
          left={attendeeEventPopup.left}
          location={
            venues.find((venue) => venue.venueId === attendeeEventPopup.event.venueId)?.address ??
            'Location unavailable'
          }
          formatDate={formatDisplayDate}
          formatTime={formatTime}
          onClose={onCloseAttendeePopup}
          onReregister={() => onReregister(attendeeEventPopup.event.eventId)}
          reregistering={reregisteringEventId === attendeeEventPopup.event.eventId}
        />
      )}

      {showEventForm && canManageEvents && (
        <Modal
          open={showEventForm}
          ariaLabel={editingEventId === null ? 'Create event' : 'Edit event'}
          onClose={onCloseEventForm}
          className="max-h-[92vh] w-full max-w-3xl overflow-y-auto rounded-xl bg-white shadow-2xl"
        >
          <EventEditorForm
            formData={formData}
            venues={venues}
            organizations={organizations}
            isEditing={editingEventId !== null}
            saving={savingEvent}
            onSubmit={onSaveEvent}
            onCancel={onCloseEventForm}
            onCancelEvent={onCancelEditingEvent}
          />
        </Modal>
      )}

      <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-text-muted">
        {canManageEvents
          ? 'Use Add Event to create an event. Open an event popup to edit or cancel it. Double-click an existing event to edit it.'
          : 'Select a calendar event to view it.'}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
export default DashboardCalendarPanel;


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/dashboard/components/DashboardSummaryCards.tsx
================================================================================
interface DashboardSummaryCardsProps {
  total: number;
  scheduled: number;
  cancelled: number;
  upcoming: number;
}

const cards = [
  { label: 'Total Events', key: 'total', color: 'var(--color-brand-primary)' },
  { label: 'Scheduled', key: 'scheduled', color: 'var(--color-accent-orange)' },
  { label: 'Cancelled', key: 'cancelled', color: 'var(--color-state-error)' },
  { label: 'Upcoming', key: 'upcoming', color: 'var(--color-state-success)' },
] as const;

function DashboardSummaryCards({
  total,
  scheduled,
  cancelled,
  upcoming,
}: DashboardSummaryCardsProps) {
  const values = { total, scheduled, cancelled, upcoming };

  return (
    <section className="mb-[30px] grid grid-cols-4 gap-5 max-[900px]:grid-cols-2 max-[700px]:grid-cols-1">
      {cards.map((card) => (
        <div
          key={card.label}
          className="glass-panel summary-accent-card p-6"
          style={{ borderTopColor: card.color }}
        >
          <span className="text-[14px] font-medium text-text-muted">{card.label}</span>
          <strong className="mt-2 block text-[30px] font-[750] text-text-primary">
            {values[card.key]}
          </strong>
        </div>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}

export default DashboardSummaryCards;

FILE: src/features/dashboard/components/UpcomingEventsPanel.tsx
================================================================================
interface UpcomingEvent {
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
  startDate: string;
}

interface UpcomingEventsPanelProps {
  events: UpcomingEvent[];
  loading: boolean;
  formatDate: (date: string) => string;
  formatTime: (date: string) => string;
}

function UpcomingEventsPanel({
  events,
  loading,
  formatDate,
  formatTime,
}: UpcomingEventsPanelProps) {
  return (
    <section className="glass-panel glass-panel-blue w-full p-6 text-text-blue-deep">
      <header className="mb-5">
        <h2 className="m-0 text-[21px] font-[750] text-text-blue-deep">Upcoming Events</h2>
        <p className="mt-[5px] text-[13px] text-text-blue-muted">Your next scheduled events</p>
      </header>

      {loading && (
        <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-text-blue-muted">Loading events...</p>
      )}
      {!loading && events.length === 0 && (
        <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-text-blue-muted">No upcoming events.</p>
      )}
      {!loading &&
        events.map((event) => {
          const eventDate = new Date(event.startDate);

          return (
            <div
              key={event.eventId}
              className="flex items-center gap-[14px] border-b border-text-blue-deep/15 py-[14px] last:border-b-0"
            >
              <div className="flex h-[60px] w-[55px] shrink-0 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-date-badge border border-blue-border bg-surface-blue">
                <strong className="text-[21px] font-extrabold leading-none text-brand-primary">
                  {eventDate.getDate()}
                </strong>
                <span className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-extrabold tracking-[0.7px] text-accent-orange">
                  {eventDate.toLocaleString('en-US', { month: 'short' }).toUpperCase()}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="m-0 font-semibold text-text-blue-deep">{event.eventName}</h3>
                <p className="m-0 mt-1 text-[12px] text-text-blue-muted">
                  {formatDate(event.startDate)}
                </p>
                <p className="m-0 text-[12px] text-text-blue-muted">
                  {formatTime(event.startDate)}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
    </section>
  );
}

export default UpcomingEventsPanel;

export { default } from './pages/DashboardPage';


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/dashboard/pages/DashboardPage.tsx
