import { apiRequest } from '../../../shared/api/apiClient';
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useAccessToken } from '@/shared/hooks/useAccessToken';
import DashboardSummaryCards from '@/features/dashboard/components/DashboardSummaryCards';
import UpcomingEventsPanel from '@/features/dashboard/components/UpcomingEventsPanel';
import DashboardCalendarPanel from '@/features/dashboard/components/DashboardCalendarPanel';
import FeedbackModal from '@/shared/components/FeedbackModal';
import type { EventFormData } from '@/features/events/schemas/eventFormSchema';
type EventStatus = 'Scheduled' | 'Cancelled' | 'Completed';
interface EventDto {
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
  startDate: string;
  endDate: string;
  budget?: number | null;
  eventType: number;
  status: EventStatus | number;
  venueId?: number | null;
  orgId?: number | null;
  isRegistrationCancelled?: boolean;
}
interface VenueOption {
  venueId: number;
  address: string;
  capacity: number;
}
interface OrganizationOption {
  orgId: number;
  name: string;
}
const emptyForm: EventFormData = {
  eventName: '',
  eventType: '0',
  startDate: '',
  endDate: '',
  venueId: '',
  orgId: '',
  budget: '0',
};
function toDateTimeLocal(value: string | Date): string {
  const date = new Date(value);
  const offset = date.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000;
  return new Date(date.getTime() - offset).toISOString().slice(0, 16);
}
function getObjectId(obj: Record<string, unknown>, ...keys: string[]): number {
  for (const key of keys) {
    const value = obj[key];
    if (typeof value === 'number' && Number.isInteger(value)) {
      return value;
    }
    if (typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() !== '' && Number.isInteger(Number(value))) {
      return Number(value);
    }
  }
  return 0;
}
function getObjectString(obj: Record<string, unknown>, ...keys: string[]): string {
  for (const key of keys) {
    const value = obj[key];
    if (typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() !== '') {
      return value;
    }
  }
  return '';
}
function DashboardPage() {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const { account, getAccessToken } = useAccessToken('Please sign in before performing this action.');
  const accountId = account?.homeAccountId;
  const roles = (account?.idTokenClaims?.roles as string[]) ?? [];
  const canManageEvents = roles.includes('Admin') || roles.includes('Employee');
  const isAttendee = roles.includes('Attendee');
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<EventDto[]>([]);
  const [venues, setVenues] = useState<VenueOption[]>([]);
  const [organizations, setOrganizations] = useState<OrganizationOption[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState('');
  const [currentDate, setCurrentDate] = useState(new Date());
  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState<Date | null>(null);
  const [showEventForm, setShowEventForm] = useState(false);
  const [editingEventId, setEditingEventId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<EventFormData>(emptyForm);
  const [savingEvent, setSavingEvent] = useState(false);
  const [attendeeEventPopup, setAttendeeEventPopup] = useState<{
    event: EventDto;
    top: number;
    left: number;
    isCompleted: boolean;
  } | null>(null);
  const [reregisteringEventId, setReregisteringEventId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const currentMonth = currentDate.getMonth();
  const currentYear = currentDate.getFullYear();
  const loadEvents = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');
      let response: Response;
      if (isAttendee) {
        if (!accountId) {
          throw new Error('Please sign in before loading registered events.');
        }
        const token = await getAccessToken();
        response = await apiRequest('/api/events/my-registrations', {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
        });
      } else {
        response = await apiRequest('/api/events');
      }
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to load events (${response.status}).`);
      }
      const data: EventDto[] = await response.json();
      setEvents(data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error loading events:', err);
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to load events.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [accountId, getAccessToken, isAttendee]);
  useEffect(() => {
    void loadEvents();
  }, [loadEvents]);
  // Load venue and organization records through their API endpoints.
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    const loadDropdowns = async () => {
      try {
        const [venueResponse, organizationResponse] = await Promise.all([
          apiRequest('/api/venues'),
          apiRequest('/api/organizations'),
        ]);
        if (!venueResponse.ok) {
          throw new Error(`Failed to load venues (${venueResponse.status}).`);
        }
        if (!organizationResponse.ok) {
          throw new Error(`Failed to load organizations (${organizationResponse.status}).`);
        }
        const venueJson: unknown = await venueResponse.json();
        const organizationJson: unknown = await organizationResponse.json();
        if (!Array.isArray(venueJson)) {
          throw new Error('The venues API did not return a list.');
        }
        if (!Array.isArray(organizationJson)) {
          throw new Error('The organizations API did not return a list.');
        }
        const venueData: VenueOption[] = venueJson
          .map((item: unknown) => {
            const obj = item as Record<string, unknown>;
            return {
              venueId: getObjectId(obj, 'venueId', 'VenueId', 'id', 'Id'),
              address: getObjectString(
                obj,
                'address',
                'Address',
                'venueName',
                'VenueName',
                'name',
                'Name',
              ),
              capacity: getObjectId(obj, 'capacity', 'Capacity'),
            };
          })
          .filter((venue) => venue.venueId > 0 && venue.address !== '');
        const organizationData: OrganizationOption[] = organizationJson
          .map((item: unknown) => {
            const obj = item as Record<string, unknown>;
            return {
              orgId: getObjectId(
                obj,
                'orgId',
                'OrgId',
                'organizationId',
                'OrganizationId',
                'id',
                'Id',
              ),
              name: getObjectString(
                obj,
                'orgName',
                'OrgName',
                'organizationName',
                'OrganizationName',
                'name',
                'Name',
              ),
            };
          })
          .filter((organization) => organization.orgId > 0 && organization.name !== '');
        if (active) {
          setVenues(venueData);
          setOrganizations(organizationData);
          if (venueData.length === 0) {
            console.warn(
              'No venue options were mapped. Check the VenueResponseDto property names and API response.',
            );
          }
          if (organizationData.length === 0) {
            console.warn(
              'No organization options were mapped. Check the OrganizationResponseDto property names and API response.',
            );
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to load dropdowns:', err);
        if (active) {
          setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to load venues and organizations.');
        }
      }
    };
    void loadDropdowns();
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    const refresh = () => void loadEvents();
    const visibility = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
        refresh();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('focus', refresh);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', visibility);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('focus', refresh);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', visibility);
    };
  }, [loadEvents]);
  const getEventStatus = (status: EventStatus | number): EventStatus => {
    if (typeof status === 'number') {
      if (status === 1) return 'Cancelled';
      if (status === 2) return 'Completed';
      return 'Scheduled';
    }
    return status;
  };
  const schedulerEvents = events
    .filter((event) => getEventStatus(event.status) !== 'Cancelled')
    .map((event) => ({
      Id: event.eventId,
      Subject: event.eventName,
      StartTime: new Date(event.startDate),
      EndTime: new Date(event.endDate),
      Status: getEventStatus(event.status),
      isRegistrationCancelled: event.isRegistrationCancelled ?? false,
    }));
  const isSameDay = (a: Date, b: Date) =>
    a.getFullYear() === b.getFullYear() &&
    a.getMonth() === b.getMonth() &&
    a.getDate() === b.getDate();
  const openCreateEvent = () => {
    if (!canManageEvents) return;
    const now = new Date();
    const start = selectedDate ? new Date(selectedDate) : now;
    if (isSameDay(start, now)) {
      start.setHours(now.getHours(), now.getMinutes(), 0, 0);
    } else {
      start.setHours(9, 0, 0, 0);
    }
    const end = new Date(start);
    end.setHours(end.getHours() + 1);
    setEditingEventId(null);
    setFormData({
      ...emptyForm,
      startDate: toDateTimeLocal(start),
      endDate: toDateTimeLocal(end),
    });
    setError('');
    setSuccessMessage('');
    setShowEventForm(true);
  };
  const openEditEvent = (eventId: number) => {
    if (!canManageEvents) return;
    const event = events.find((item) => item.eventId === eventId);
    if (!event) {
      setError('Event not found. Please refresh and try again.');
      return;
    }
    const status = getEventStatus(event.status);
    if (status === 'Cancelled' || status === 'Completed') {
      setError(
        status === 'Completed'
          ? 'Completed events cannot be edited.'
          : 'Cancelled events cannot be edited.',
      );
      return;
    }
    setEditingEventId(eventId);
    setFormData({
      eventName: event.eventName,
      eventType: String(event.eventType ?? 0),
      startDate: toDateTimeLocal(event.startDate),
      endDate: toDateTimeLocal(event.endDate),
      venueId: event.venueId == null ? '' : String(event.venueId),
      orgId: event.orgId == null ? '' : String(event.orgId),
      budget: event.budget == null ? '' : String(event.budget),
    });
    setError('');
    setShowEventForm(true);
  };
  const closeEventForm = () => {
    if (savingEvent) return;
    setShowEventForm(false);
    setEditingEventId(null);
    setFormData(emptyForm);
  };
  const saveCalendarEvent = async (values: EventFormData) => {
    if (!canManageEvents) {
      setError('You do not have permission to manage events.');
      return;
    }
    if (editingEventId !== null) {
      const eventBeingEdited = events.find((item) => item.eventId === editingEventId);
      if (!eventBeingEdited || getEventStatus(eventBeingEdited.status) !== 'Scheduled') {
        setError('Only scheduled events can be edited.');
        return;
      }
    }
    const startDate = new Date(values.startDate);
    const endDate = new Date(values.endDate);
    const venueId = Number(values.venueId);
    const orgId = values.orgId ? Number(values.orgId) : null;
    const budget = Number(values.budget);
    const eventType = Number(values.eventType);
    try {
      setSavingEvent(true);
      setError('');
      const token = await getAccessToken();
      const isEditing = editingEventId !== null;
      const payload = {
        eventName: values.eventName.trim(),
        startDate: startDate.toISOString(),
        endDate: endDate.toISOString(),
        venueId,
        budget,
        eventType,
        orgId,
      };
      const response = await apiRequest(
        isEditing ? `/api/events/${editingEventId}` : '/api/events',
        {
          method: isEditing ? 'PUT' : 'POST',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload),
        },
      );
      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();
        throw new Error(
          message || `Unable to ${isEditing ? 'update' : 'create'} event (${response.status}).`,
        );
      }
      setSuccessMessage(
        editingEventId === null
          ? 'Event created successfully.'
          : 'Event changes saved successfully.',
      );
      setShowEventForm(false);
      setEditingEventId(null);
      setFormData(emptyForm);
      await loadEvents();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Save event failed:', err);
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to save event.');
    } finally {
      setSavingEvent(false);
    }
  };
  const cancelCalendarEvent = async (eventId: number) => {
    if (!canManageEvents) {
      setError('You do not have permission to cancel events.');
      return;
    }
    const event = events.find((item) => item.eventId === eventId);
    if (!event) {
      setError('Event not found. Refresh the calendar and try again.');
      return;
    }
    if (getEventStatus(event.status) === 'Cancelled') {
      setError('This event is already cancelled.');
      return;
    }
    if (!window.confirm(`Cancel "${event.eventName}"? The event will remain in the database.`)) {
      return;
    }
    try {
      setError('');
      const token = await getAccessToken();
      const response = await apiRequest(`/api/events/${eventId}/cancel`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
      });
      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();
        throw new Error(message || `Unable to cancel event (${response.status}).`);
      }
      setShowEventForm(false);
      setEditingEventId(null);
      setFormData(emptyForm);
      await loadEvents();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Cancel event failed:', err);
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to cancel event.');
    }
  };
  // Use explicit popup actions rather than using Syncfusion's delete behavior.
  const handlePopupOpen = (args: {
    type: string;
    data?: Record<string, unknown>;
    cancel: boolean;
    target?: Element;
  }) => {
    if (args.type !== 'QuickInfo') return;
    const eventId = Number(args.data?.Id ?? args.data?.EventId ?? args.data?.eventId);
    // QuickInfo without an existing event is the scheduler's empty-cell
    // create popup. Event creation should only happen through Add Event.
    if (!Number.isInteger(eventId) || eventId <= 0) {
      args.cancel = true;
      return;
    }
    const event = events.find((item) => item.eventId === eventId);
    const showDetailsInApp =
      isAttendee ||
      (canManageEvents && event !== undefined && getEventStatus(event.status) === 'Completed');
    if (showDetailsInApp) {
      args.cancel = true;
      if (event) {
        const target = args.target as HTMLElement | undefined;
        const rect = target?.getBoundingClientRect();
        const popupWidth = 320;
        const popupHeight = 230;
        const left = rect
          ? rect.right + popupWidth + 16 <= window.innerWidth
            ? rect.right + 8
            : Math.max(16, rect.left - popupWidth - 8)
          : 16;
        const top = rect
          ? Math.min(Math.max(16, rect.top), window.innerHeight - popupHeight - 16)
          : 16;
        setAttendeeEventPopup({
          event,
          top,
          left,
          isCompleted: getEventStatus(event.status) === 'Completed',
        });
      } else {
        setAttendeeEventPopup(null);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (!canManageEvents) return;
    window.setTimeout(() => {
      const popup = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.e-quick-popup-wrapper');
      if (!popup) return;
      popup.classList.add('glass-popup');
      const event = events.find((item) => item.eventId === eventId);
      const removePopupActions = () => {
        popup
          .querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
            '.e-event-edit, .e-edit, .e-event-delete, .e-delete, [title="Edit"], [aria-label="Edit"], [title="Delete"], [aria-label="Delete"]',
          )
          .forEach((button) => button.remove());
        popup.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('button, .e-btn').forEach((button) => {
          if (['edit', 'delete'].includes(button.textContent?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? '')) {
            button.remove();
          }
        });
      };
      removePopupActions();
      const popupObserver = new MutationObserver(removePopupActions);
      popupObserver.observe(popup, { childList: true, subtree: true });
      window.setTimeout(() => popupObserver.disconnect(), 5000);
      if (!event || getEventStatus(event.status) !== 'Scheduled') return;
      if (popup.querySelector('[data-custom-cancel-event="true"]')) {
        return;
      }
      const cancelButton = document.createElement('button');
      cancelButton.type = 'button';
      cancelButton.textContent = 'Cancel Event';
      cancelButton.setAttribute('data-custom-cancel-event', 'true');
      cancelButton.className = 'e-btn e-flat e-primary';
      cancelButton.style.margin = '8px';
      cancelButton.style.color = 'var(--color-state-error)';
      cancelButton.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        void cancelCalendarEvent(eventId);
      });
      const footer = popup.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.e-footer-content');
      (footer ?? popup).appendChild(cancelButton);
    }, 0);
  };
  const monthEvents = events.filter((event) => {
    if (getEventStatus(event.status) === 'Cancelled') return false;
    const date = new Date(event.startDate);
    return date.getMonth() === currentMonth && date.getFullYear() === currentYear;
  });
  const scheduledEvents = events.filter(
    (event) => getEventStatus(event.status) === 'Scheduled' && !event.isRegistrationCancelled,
  );
  const cancelledEvents = events.filter((event) => getEventStatus(event.status) === 'Cancelled');
  const upcomingEvents = events
    .filter(
      (event) =>
        getEventStatus(event.status) === 'Scheduled' &&
        !event.isRegistrationCancelled &&
        new Date(event.startDate) >= new Date(),
    )
    .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.startDate).getTime() - new Date(b.startDate).getTime())
    .slice(0, 5);
  const applySelectedDateStyle = useCallback(() => {
    const cells = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.dashboard-calendar .e-work-cells');
    cells.forEach((cell) => {
      cell.style.outline = '';
      cell.style.outlineOffset = '';
      cell.style.boxShadow = '';
      cell.style.backgroundColor = '';
      const dateValue = cell.getAttribute('data-date');
      if (!selectedDate || !dateValue) return;
      if (isSameDay(new Date(Number(dateValue)), selectedDate)) {
        cell.style.outline = '2px solid var(--color-purple)';
        cell.style.outlineOffset = '-2px';
        cell.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--color-surface-lavender)';
      }
    });
  }, [selectedDate]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const timeoutId = window.setTimeout(applySelectedDateStyle, 50);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
  }, [applySelectedDateStyle, currentDate, loading]);
  const handleCellClick = (args: { startTime: Date }) => {
    setSelectedDate(args.startTime);
  };
  // Prevent double-clicking empty cells from opening the default editor.
  const handleCellDoubleClick = (args: { cancel: boolean }) => {
    args.cancel = true;
  };
  // Double-clicking an existing event opens our custom edit form.
  const handleEventDoubleClick = (args: {
    cancel: boolean;
    event?: unknown;
    data?: Record<string, unknown>;
  }) => {
    args.cancel = true;
    const eventData =
      args.event && typeof args.event === 'object' && !Array.isArray(args.event)
        ? (args.event as Record<string, unknown>)
        : undefined;
    const eventId = Number(
      eventData?.Id ?? eventData?.EventId ?? eventData?.eventId ?? args.data?.Id,
    );
    const event = events.find((item) => item.eventId === eventId);
    if (
      Number.isInteger(eventId) &&
      eventId > 0 &&
      event &&
      getEventStatus(event.status) === 'Scheduled'
    ) {
      openEditEvent(eventId);
    }
  };
  const handleEventRendered = (args: { data: Record<string, unknown>; element: HTMLElement }) => {
    if (args.data?.isRegistrationCancelled) {
      args.element.style.setProperty(
        'background-color',
        'var(--color-surface-orange-light)',
        'important',
      );
      args.element.style.setProperty('border-color', 'var(--color-orange-border)', 'important');
      args.element.style.setProperty('color', 'var(--color-text-orange-deep)', 'important');
      args.element.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('*').forEach((child) => {
        child.style.setProperty('color', 'var(--color-text-orange-deep)', 'important');
      });
      args.element.style.setProperty('opacity', '1', 'important');
      return;
    }
    if (args.data?.Status !== 'Completed') return;
    args.element.style.setProperty(
      'background-color',
      'var(--color-surface-lavender-light)',
      'important',
    );
    args.element.style.setProperty('border-color', 'var(--color-lavender-border)', 'important');
    args.element.style.setProperty('color', 'var(--color-purple-strong)', 'important');
    args.element.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('*').forEach((child) => {
      child.style.setProperty('color', 'var(--color-purple-strong)', 'important');
    });
    args.element.style.setProperty('opacity', '1', 'important');
  };
  const reregisterForEvent = async (eventId: number) => {
    try {
      setReregisteringEventId(eventId);
      setError('');
      const token = await getAccessToken();
      const response = await apiRequest(`/api/events/${eventId}/register`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
      });
      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();
        throw new Error(message || 'Failed to re-register for this event.');
      }
      await loadEvents();
      setAttendeeEventPopup(null);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Event re-registration failed:', err);
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to re-register for this event.');
    } finally {
      setReregisteringEventId(null);
    }
  };
  const handleRenderCell = (args: { element: HTMLElement; date?: Date; type: string }) => {
    if (args.type !== 'workCells' || !selectedDate || !args.date) return;
    if (isSameDay(args.date, selectedDate)) {
      args.element.style.boxShadow = 'inset 0 0 0 2px var(--color-purple)';
      args.element.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--color-surface-lavender)';
    }
  };
  const formatTime = (date: string) =>
    new Date(date).toLocaleTimeString([], {
      hour: 'numeric',
      minute: '2-digit',
    });
  const formatDisplayDate = (date: string) =>
    new Date(date).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
      month: 'short',
      day: 'numeric',
      year: 'numeric',
    });
  const userName = account?.name || account?.username || 'User';
  return (
    <div className="page-canvas min-h-[calc(100vh-68px)] w-full px-[46px] pt-9 pb-[60px] max-[1150px]:px-[25px] max-[700px]:px-[15px]">
      <header className="relative mx-auto mb-8 w-full max-w-[1400px] overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-white/70 bg-white/30 px-6 py-6 text-center shadow-panel backdrop-blur-xl">
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-medium tracking-wide text-text-muted sm:text-base">{t('dashboard.welcomeBack')}</p>

        <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight text-text-primary sm:text-4xl">
          {t('dashboard.welcome', { name: userName })}
        </p>
        <span className="page-accent page-accent-centered mt-4 w-16" aria-hidden="true" />
      </header>

      <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1400px]">
        <DashboardSummaryCards
          total={events.length}
          scheduled={scheduledEvents.length}
          cancelled={cancelledEvents.length}
          upcoming={upcomingEvents.length}
        />

        {error && (
          <div className="mb-5 flex items-start justify-between gap-3 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
            <span>{error}</span>

            <button type="button" onClick={() => setError('')} aria-label="Dismiss error">
              ×
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        <section className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.65fr)_minmax(320px,0.85fr)] items-start gap-6 max-[1150px]:grid-cols-1">
          <DashboardCalendarPanel
            monthEventCount={monthEvents.length}
            selectedDate={selectedDate}
            currentDate={currentDate}
            schedulerEvents={schedulerEvents}
            loading={loading}
            canManageEvents={canManageEvents}
            isAttendee={isAttendee}
            venues={venues}
            organizations={organizations}
            attendeeEventPopup={attendeeEventPopup}
            showEventForm={showEventForm}
            editingEventId={editingEventId}
            savingEvent={savingEvent}
            formData={formData}
            onSetCurrentDate={setCurrentDate}
            onClearSelectedDate={() => setSelectedDate(null)}
            onCreateEvent={openCreateEvent}
            onEventRendered={handleEventRendered}
            onCellClick={handleCellClick}
            onCellDoubleClick={handleCellDoubleClick}
            onEventDoubleClick={handleEventDoubleClick}
            onPopupOpen={handlePopupOpen}
            onRenderCell={handleRenderCell}
            onCloseAttendeePopup={() => setAttendeeEventPopup(null)}
            onReregister={reregisterForEvent}
            reregisteringEventId={reregisteringEventId}
            formatDisplayDate={formatDisplayDate}
            formatTime={formatTime}
            onCloseEventForm={closeEventForm}
            onCancelEditingEvent={() => {
              if (editingEventId !== null) void cancelCalendarEvent(editingEventId);
            }}
            onSaveEvent={saveCalendarEvent}
          />

          <UpcomingEventsPanel
            events={upcomingEvents}
            loading={loading}
            formatDate={formatDisplayDate}
            formatTime={formatTime}
          />
        </section>
      </main>
      <FeedbackModal
        open={Boolean(successMessage)}
        title="Event saved"
        message={successMessage}
        onClose={() => setSuccessMessage('')}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
export default DashboardPage;

import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { eventFormSchema, type EventFormData } from '@/features/events/schemas/eventFormSchema';
import FormField from '@/shared/components/FormField';

interface VenueOption {
  venueId: number;
  address: string;
  capacity: number;
}

interface OrganizationOption {
  orgId: number;
  name: string;
}

interface EventEditorFormProps {
  formData: EventFormData;
  venues: VenueOption[];
  organizations: OrganizationOption[];
  isEditing: boolean;
  saving: boolean;
  onSubmit: (values: EventFormData) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
  onCancelEvent?: () => void;
}

const inputClass =
  'w-full rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-text-primary outline-none transition placeholder:text-text-placeholder focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-primary/10';

function EventEditorForm({
  formData,
  venues,
  organizations,
  isEditing,
  saving,
  onSubmit,
  onCancel,
  onCancelEvent,
}: EventEditorFormProps) {
  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    reset,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<EventFormData>({
    resolver: zodResolver(eventFormSchema),
    defaultValues: formData,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    reset(formData);
  }, [formData, reset]);

  return (
    <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-white/35 bg-transparent shadow-none">
      <header className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border-subtle px-6 py-5">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-text-primary">
          {isEditing ? 'Edit Event' : 'Create Event'}
        </h2>
      </header>

      <form className="space-y-5 p-6" onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
          <FormField label="Event Name" htmlFor="eventName" className="gap-2" required>
            <input
              className={inputClass}
              id="eventName"
              required
              type="text"
              placeholder="Enter event name"
              aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.eventName)}
              aria-describedby={errors.eventName ? 'eventName-error' : undefined}
              {...register('eventName')}
            />
            {errors.eventName && (
              <span id="eventName-error" className="text-xs text-red-700">
                {errors.eventName.message}
              </span>
            )}
          </FormField>

          <FormField label="Event Type" htmlFor="eventType" className="gap-2" required>
            <select
              className={inputClass}
              id="eventType"
              required
              aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.eventType)}
              aria-describedby={errors.eventType ? 'eventType-error' : undefined}
              {...register('eventType')}
            >
              <option value="">Select event type</option>
              <option value="0">Conference</option>
              <option value="1">Workshop</option>
              <option value="2">Webinar</option>
              <option value="3">Cultural Fest</option>
              <option value="4">Meeting</option>
              <option value="5">Product Launch</option>
            </select>
            {errors.eventType && (
              <span id="eventType-error" className="text-xs text-red-700">
                {errors.eventType.message}
              </span>
            )}
          </FormField>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
          <FormField label="Start Date and Time" htmlFor="startDate" className="gap-2" required>
            <input
              className={inputClass}
              id="startDate"
              required
              type="datetime-local"
              aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.startDate)}
              aria-describedby={errors.startDate ? 'startDate-error' : undefined}
              {...register('startDate')}
            />
            {errors.startDate && (
              <span id="startDate-error" className="text-xs text-red-700">
                {errors.startDate.message}
              </span>
            )}
          </FormField>
          <FormField label="End Date and Time" htmlFor="endDate" className="gap-2" required>
            <input
              className={inputClass}
              id="endDate"
              required
              type="datetime-local"
              aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.endDate)}
              aria-describedby={errors.endDate ? 'endDate-error' : undefined}
              {...register('endDate')}
            />
            {errors.endDate && (
              <span id="endDate-error" className="text-xs text-red-700">
                {errors.endDate.message}
              </span>
            )}
          </FormField>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
          <FormField label="Venue" htmlFor="venue" className="gap-2" required>
            <select
              className={inputClass}
              id="venue"
              required
              aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.venueId)}
              aria-describedby={errors.venueId ? 'venue-error' : undefined}
              {...register('venueId')}
            >
              <option value="">Select venue</option>
              {venues.map((venue) => (
                <option key={venue.venueId} value={venue.venueId}>
                  {venue.address} - Capacity: {venue.capacity}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            {errors.venueId && (
              <span id="venue-error" className="text-xs text-red-700">
                {errors.venueId.message}
              </span>
            )}
          </FormField>
          <FormField label="Organization" htmlFor="organization" className="gap-2">
            <select className={inputClass} id="organization" {...register('orgId')}>
              <option value="">No organization</option>
              {organizations.map((organization) => (
                <option key={organization.orgId} value={organization.orgId}>
                  {organization.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </FormField>
        </div>

        <FormField label="Budget" htmlFor="budget" className="gap-2" required>
          <input
            className={inputClass}
            id="budget"
              required
            type="number"
            min="0"
            placeholder="Enter budget"
            aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.budget)}
            aria-describedby={errors.budget ? 'budget-error' : undefined}
            {...register('budget')}
          />
          {errors.budget && (
            <span id="budget-error" className="text-xs text-red-700">
              {errors.budget.message}
            </span>
          )}
        </FormField>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-2">
          {isEditing && onCancelEvent && (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-red-700 transition hover:bg-red-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              onClick={onCancelEvent}
              disabled={saving}
            >
              Cancel Event
            </button>
          )}
          <div className="ml-auto flex gap-3">
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              disabled={saving}
            >
              {saving ? 'Saving...' : isEditing ? 'Update Event' : 'Create Event'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </form>
    </section>
  );
}

export default EventEditorForm;

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import StatusBadge from '@/shared/components/StatusBadge';

type EventStatus = 0 | 1 | 2;
type EventType = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5;

interface EventListItem {
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
  startDate: string;
  endDate: string;
  budget: number | null;
  eventType: EventType;
  status: EventStatus;
  venueId: number | null;
  orgId: number | null;
}

interface EventListPanelProps {
  events: EventListItem[];
  loading: boolean;
  searchValue: string;
  statusFilter: string;
  canManageEvents: boolean;
  isAttendee: boolean;
  registeringEventId: number | null;
  cancellingRegistrationEventId: number | null;
  onSearchChange: (value: string) => void;
  onStatusFilterChange: (value: string) => void;
  isRegistered: (eventId: number) => boolean;
  isRegistrationCancelled: (eventId: number) => boolean;
  getEventTypeText: (eventType: EventType) => string;
  formatDate: (date: string) => string;
  onAssign: (event: EventListItem) => void;
  onEdit: (eventId: number) => void;
  onCancel: (eventId: number) => void;
  onRegister: (eventId: number) => void;
  onCancelRegistration: (eventId: number) => void;
}

function EventListPanel({
  events,
  loading,
  searchValue,
  statusFilter,
  canManageEvents,
  isAttendee,
  registeringEventId,
  cancellingRegistrationEventId,
  onSearchChange,
  onStatusFilterChange,
  isRegistered,
  isRegistrationCancelled,
  getEventTypeText,
  formatDate,
  onAssign,
  onEdit,
  onCancel,
  onRegister,
  onCancelRegistration,
}: EventListPanelProps) {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    setNow(Date.now());
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 60_000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  const emptyMessage =
    statusFilter === 'upcoming'
      ? 'There are no upcoming events.'
      : statusFilter === 'ongoing'
        ? 'There are no ongoing events.'
        : statusFilter === 'completed'
          ? 'There are no past or completed events.'
          : statusFilter === 'cancelled'
            ? 'There are no cancelled events.'
            : canManageEvents
              ? 'Create your first event to see it here.'
              : 'There are no events matching your search.';

  return (
    <section className="glass-panel mb-6 w-full overflow-hidden">
      <header className="border-b border-border-subtle px-6 py-5">
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-text-primary">Events</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-muted">
          {canManageEvents
            ? 'View and manage your events.'
            : 'View available events and register for the ones you want to attend.'}
        </p>
      </header>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-border-subtle p-6 md:flex-row">
        <input
          className="w-full rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-text-primary outline-none transition placeholder:text-text-placeholder focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-primary/10"
          type="text"
          placeholder="Search events..."
          value={searchValue}
          onChange={(event) => onSearchChange(event.target.value)}
        />
        <select
          className="w-full rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-text-primary outline-none transition focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-primary/10"
          value={statusFilter}
          onChange={(event) => onStatusFilterChange(event.target.value)}
        >
          <option value="upcoming">Upcoming Events</option>
          <option value="ongoing">Ongoing Events</option>
          <option value="all">All Statuses</option>
          <option value="completed">Past / Completed Events</option>
          <option value="cancelled">Cancelled Events</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="table-grid min-w-full border-collapse text-left text-sm">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              {['Event Name', 'Type', 'Start Date', 'End Date', 'Status', 'Actions'].map(
                (heading) => (
                  <th
                    key={heading}
                    className="bg-surface-subtle px-5 py-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-text-muted"
                  >
                    {heading}
                  </th>
                ),
              )}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {loading ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={6}>
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 py-12 text-center">
                    <h3>Loading events...</h3>
                  </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : events.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={6}>
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 py-12 text-center">
                    <h3>No events found</h3>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-text-muted">{emptyMessage}</p>
                  </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              events.map((event) => {
                const registered = isRegistered(event.eventId);
                const registrationCancelled = isRegistrationCancelled(event.eventId);
                const start = new Date(event.startDate).getTime();
                const end = new Date(event.endDate).getTime();
                const displayStatus =
                  event.status === 1
                    ? 'Cancelled'
                    : event.status === 2
                      ? 'Completed'
                      : end < now
                        ? 'Past'
                        : start <= now
                          ? 'Ongoing'
                          : 'Upcoming';
                const eventIsPast = displayStatus === 'Past' || displayStatus === 'Completed';
                return (
                  <tr key={event.eventId}>
                    <td className="border-t border-border-subtle px-5 py-4 text-sm text-text-secondary">
                      {event.eventName}
                    </td>
                    <td className="border-t border-border-subtle px-5 py-4 text-sm text-text-secondary">
                      {getEventTypeText(event.eventType)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="border-t border-border-subtle px-5 py-4 text-sm text-text-secondary">
                      {formatDate(event.startDate)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="border-t border-border-subtle px-5 py-4 text-sm text-text-secondary">
                      {formatDate(event.endDate)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="border-t border-border-subtle px-5 py-4 text-sm text-text-secondary">
                      <StatusBadge status={displayStatus} />
                    </td>
                    <td className="border-t border-border-subtle px-5 py-4 text-sm text-text-secondary">
                      {canManageEvents && (
                        <>
                          {event.status !== 1 && event.status !== 2 && !eventIsPast && (
                            <>
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                className="rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover"
                                onClick={() => onAssign(event)}
                              >
                                Assign Task
                              </button>
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                className="ml-2 rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary transition hover:bg-surface-subtle"
                                onClick={() => onEdit(event.eventId)}
                              >
                                Edit
                              </button>
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                className="ml-2 rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary transition hover:bg-surface-subtle"
                                onClick={() => onCancel(event.eventId)}
                              >
                                Cancel
                              </button>
                            </>
                          )}
                          {event.status === 1 && (
                            <span className="text-sm text-text-muted">Cancelled</span>
                          )}
                          {event.status === 2 && (
                            <span className="text-sm text-text-muted">Completed</span>
                          )}
                        </>
                      )}

                      {isAttendee && (
                        <>
                          {registered ? (
                            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                              <span className="inline-flex rounded-lg border border-green-200 bg-green-50 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-green-700">
                                Registered
                              </span>
                              {event.status === 0 && !eventIsPast && (
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  className="rounded-lg border border-orange-200 bg-orange-50 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-orange-800 transition hover:bg-orange-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                                  disabled={cancellingRegistrationEventId === event.eventId}
                                  onClick={() => onCancelRegistration(event.eventId)}
                                >
                                  {cancellingRegistrationEventId === event.eventId
                                    ? 'Cancelling...'
                                    : 'Cancel Registration'}
                                </button>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          ) : registrationCancelled ? (
                            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                              <span className="inline-flex rounded-lg border border-orange-200 bg-orange-50 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-orange-800">
                                Cancelled
                              </span>
                              {event.status === 0 && !eventIsPast && (
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  className="rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                                  disabled={registeringEventId === event.eventId}
                                  onClick={() => onRegister(event.eventId)}
                                >
                                  {registeringEventId === event.eventId
                                    ? 'Registering...'
                                    : 'Re-register'}
                                </button>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          ) : event.status === 0 && displayStatus === 'Upcoming' ? (
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              className="rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                              disabled={registeringEventId === event.eventId}
                              onClick={() => onRegister(event.eventId)}
                            >
                              {registeringEventId === event.eventId ? 'Registering...' : 'Register'}
                            </button>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-sm text-text-muted">Not available</span>
                          )}
                        </>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export default EventListPanel;

FILE: src/features/events/components/EventTaskAssignmentPanel.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, type Dispatch, type SetStateAction } from 'react';
import StatusBadge from '@/shared/components/StatusBadge';
import FormField from '@/shared/components/FormField';

interface EmployeeOption {
  employeeId: number;
  name: string;
  jobTitle: string | null;
}

interface StaffAssignment {
  assignmentId: number;
  employeeId?: number;
  name: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  task: string;
  deadline: string | null;
  status: string | number;
}

interface EventTaskAssignmentPanelProps {
  eventName: string;
  employees: EmployeeOption[];
  assignedStaff: StaffAssignment[];
  selectedEmployeeId: string;
  setSelectedEmployeeId: Dispatch<SetStateAction<string>>;
  task: string;
  setTask: Dispatch<SetStateAction<string>>;
  deadline: string;
  setDeadline: Dispatch<SetStateAction<string>>;
  assigning: boolean;
  onAssign: () => void;
  onReassign?: (assignment: StaffAssignment, employeeId: number) => void | Promise<void>;
}

function EventTaskAssignmentPanel({
  eventName,
  employees,
  assignedStaff,
  selectedEmployeeId,
  setSelectedEmployeeId,
  task,
  setTask,
  deadline,
  setDeadline,
  assigning,
  onAssign,
  onReassign,
}: EventTaskAssignmentPanelProps) {
  const [reassignmentEmployeeIds, setReassignmentEmployeeIds] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const getStatusLabel = (status: string | number) => ({ '0': 'Pending', '1': 'In Progress', '2': 'Completed', '3': 'Declined' }[String(status)] ?? String(status));
  const isDeclined = (status: string | number) => getStatusLabel(status).toLowerCase() === 'declined';
  const isAlreadyReassigned = (assignment: StaffAssignment) =>
    assignedStaff.some((other) => {
      const sameTask = other.task.trim().toLowerCase() === assignment.task.trim().toLowerCase();
      const sameDeadline = (other.deadline ? new Date(other.deadline).getTime() : null) ===
        (assignment.deadline ? new Date(assignment.deadline).getTime() : null);
      return other.assignmentId !== assignment.assignmentId &&
        other.employeeId !== assignment.employeeId &&
        !isDeclined(other.status) &&
        sameTask &&
        sameDeadline;
    });
  const handleReassign = async (assignment: StaffAssignment) => {
    const employeeId = Number(reassignmentEmployeeIds[assignment.assignmentId]);
    if (!employeeId || !onReassign) return;
    await onReassign(assignment, employeeId);
    setReassignmentEmployeeIds((current) => ({ ...current, [assignment.assignmentId]: '' }));
  };
  return (
    <section className="mb-6 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-white/35 bg-transparent shadow-none">
      <header className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border-subtle px-6 py-5">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-text-primary">Assign Employee Task</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-muted">Event: {eventName}</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="space-y-5 p-6">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
          <FormField label="Employee" htmlFor="assignedEmployee" required>
            <select
              id="assignedEmployee"
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-text-primary outline-none transition focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-primary/10"
              value={selectedEmployeeId}
              onChange={(event) => setSelectedEmployeeId(event.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="">Select employee</option>
              {employees.map((employee) => (
                <option key={employee.employeeId} value={employee.employeeId}>
                  {employee.name}
                  {employee.jobTitle ? ` - ${employee.jobTitle}` : ''}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </FormField>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary" htmlFor="taskDeadline">
              Deadline
            </label>
            <input
              id="taskDeadline"
              type="datetime-local"
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-text-primary outline-none transition focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-primary/10"
              value={deadline}
              onChange={(event) => setDeadline(event.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <FormField label="Task" htmlFor="employeeTask" required>
          <textarea
            id="employeeTask"
            rows={3}
            placeholder="Enter the task assigned to this employee"
            className="w-full resize-none rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-text-primary outline-none transition placeholder:text-text-placeholder focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-primary/10"
            value={task}
            onChange={(event) => setTask(event.target.value)}
          />
        </FormField>

        <div className="flex justify-end">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
            disabled={assigning}
            onClick={onAssign}
          >
            {assigning ? 'Assigning...' : 'Assign Task'}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="border-t border-border-subtle pt-5">
          <h3 className="mb-4 text-base font-semibold text-text-primary">Assigned Employees</h3>
          {assignedStaff.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-text-muted">
              No employees have been assigned to this event yet.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-border-default">
              <table className="min-w-full text-left text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="bg-surface-subtle">
                    {['Employee', 'Task', 'Deadline', 'Status', 'Action'].map((heading) => (
                      <th
                        key={heading}
                        className="px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-text-muted"
                      >
                        {heading}
                      </th>
                    ))}
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {assignedStaff.map((assignment) => (
                    <tr key={assignment.assignmentId} className="border-t border-border-subtle">
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        <p className="font-medium text-text-primary">{assignment.name}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-text-muted">{assignment.jobTitle}</p>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-text-secondary">{assignment.task}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-text-secondary">
                        {assignment.deadline
                          ? new Date(assignment.deadline).toLocaleString()
                          : 'No deadline'}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        <StatusBadge context="task" status={getStatusLabel(assignment.status)} />
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        {isDeclined(assignment.status) && !isAlreadyReassigned(assignment) && (
                          <div className="flex min-w-[17rem] items-center gap-2">
                            <select
                              aria-label={`Select employee to reassign ${assignment.task}`}
                              className="min-w-0 flex-1 rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-text-primary"
                              value={reassignmentEmployeeIds[assignment.assignmentId] ?? ''}
                              onChange={(event) =>
                                setReassignmentEmployeeIds((current) => ({
                                  ...current,
                                  [assignment.assignmentId]: event.target.value,
                                }))
                              }
                            >
                              <option value="">Select employee</option>
                              {employees
                                .filter((employee) => employee.employeeId !== assignment.employeeId)
                                .map((employee) => (
                                  <option key={employee.employeeId} value={employee.employeeId}>
                                    {employee.name}
                                  </option>
                                ))}
                            </select>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              className="rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                              disabled={assigning || !reassignmentEmployeeIds[assignment.assignmentId] || !onReassign}
                              onClick={() => void handleReassign(assignment)}
                            >
                              Assign
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export default EventTaskAssignmentPanel;


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/events/index.ts
