export { default } from './pages/EventsPage';


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/events/pages/EventsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { apiRequest } from '../../../shared/api/apiClient';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useAccessToken } from '@/shared/hooks/useAccessToken';
import { useNavigate, useParams, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import PageHeader from '@/shared/components/PageHeader';
import EventTaskAssignmentPanel from '@/features/events/components/EventTaskAssignmentPanel';
import EventEditorForm from '@/features/events/components/EventEditorForm';
import EventListPanel from '@/features/events/components/EventListPanel';
import Modal from '@/shared/components/Modal';
import FeedbackModal from '@/shared/components/FeedbackModal';
import type { EventFormData } from '../schemas/eventFormSchema';

type EventStatus = 0 | 1 | 2;
// 0 = Scheduled
// 1 = Cancelled
// 2 = Completed

type EventType = 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5;
// 0 = Conference
// 1 = Workshop
// 2 = Webinar
// 3 = Cultural Fest
// 4 = Meeting
// 5 = Product Launch

const API_URL = '/api';

interface EventResponse {
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
  startDate: string;
  endDate: string;
  budget: number | null;
  eventType: EventType;
  status: EventStatus;
  venueId: number | null;
  orgId: number | null;
  isRegistrationCancelled?: boolean;
}

interface Venue {
  venueId: number;
  address: string;
  capacity: number;
}

interface Organization {
  orgId: number;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  contactPerson: string;
}

interface Employee {
  employeeId: number;
  name: string;
  jobTitle: string | null;
  email: string;
  orgId: number;
}

interface StaffAssignment {
  assignmentId: number;
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
  employeeId: number;
  name: string;
  jobTitle: string;
  task: string;
  deadline: string | null;
  status: string | number;
}

function EventsPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { eventId: routeEventId } = useParams<{ eventId: string }>();
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();

  const { account, getAccessToken } = useAccessToken();

  // Stabilize account ID dependencies
  const accountId = account?.homeAccountId;

  const [venues, setVenues] = useState<Venue[]>([]);

  const [organizations, setOrganizations] = useState<Organization[]>([]);

  const [employees, setEmployees] = useState<Employee[]>([]);

  const [showAssignForm, setShowAssignForm] = useState(false);

  const [assigningEvent, setAssigningEvent] = useState<EventResponse | null>(null);

  const [assignedStaff, setAssignedStaff] = useState<StaffAssignment[]>([]);

  const [selectedEmployeeId, setSelectedEmployeeId] = useState('');

  const [task, setTask] = useState('');

  const [deadline, setDeadline] = useState('');

  const [assigning, setAssigning] = useState(false);
  // ATTENDEE REGISTRATION STATE
  const [registeredEventIds, setRegisteredEventIds] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [cancelledRegistrationEventIds, setCancelledRegistrationEventIds] = useState<number[]>([]);
  // ROLE
  const roles = (account?.idTokenClaims?.roles as string[]) ?? [];

  const isAdmin = roles.includes('Admin');
  const isEmployee = roles.includes('Employee');
  const isAttendee = roles.includes('Attendee');

  const canManageEvents = isAdmin || isEmployee;

  const [showCreateForm, setShowCreateForm] = useState(false);

  const [editingEventId, setEditingEventId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [searchText, setSearchText] = useState('');
  // STATUS FILTER
  // Default is Scheduled.
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('upcoming');

  const eventsQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['events', accountId],
    queryFn: async (): Promise<EventResponse[]> => {
      const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events`);
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to load events. Status: ${response.status}`);
      }

      return response.json();
    },
  });
  const events = eventsQuery.data ?? [];

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const [registeringEventId, setRegisteringEventId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState('');

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<EventFormData>({
    eventName: '',
    startDate: '',
    endDate: '',
    venueId: '',
    budget: '0',
    eventType: '',
    orgId: '',
  });
  // API URL
  // AUTHORIZATION HEADER
  const getAuthHeaders = async (): Promise<HeadersInit> => {
    const token = await getAccessToken();
    return { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + token };
  };
  // LOAD ALL DATA
  async function loadData() {
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');
    // LOAD EVENTS FIRST
    // Events drive this page
    // Keep event loading independent
    try {
      await loadEvents();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load events:', error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to load events.');
    }
    // LOAD VENUES
    try {
      await loadVenues();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load venues:', error);
    }
    // LOAD ORGANIZATIONS
    try {
      await loadOrganizations();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load organizations:', error);
    }
    // LOAD EMPLOYEES
    if (canManageEvents) {
      try {
        await loadEmployees();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to load employees:', error);
      }
    }
    // LOAD ATTENDEE REGISTRATIONS
    if (isAttendee) {
      try {
        await loadMyRegistrations();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to load registrations:', error);
      }
    }

    setLoading(false);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadData();
    // These values trigger data loading
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [accountId, isAttendee, canManageEvents, statusFilter]);
  // GET EVENTS
  // Scheduled:
  // GET /api/events?upcomingOnly=true
  // Past:
  // GET /api/events?upcomingOnly=false
  // All:
  // GET /api/events
  // Cancelled:
  // GET /api/events
  // Then frontend filters cancelled events.
  async function loadEvents() {
    const result = await eventsQuery.refetch();
    if (result.error) {
      throw result.error;
    }
  }
  // GET MY REGISTERED EVENTS
  // GET /api/events/my-registrations
  async function loadMyRegistrations() {
    const authHeaders = await getAuthHeaders();

    const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events/my-registrations`, {
      headers: authHeaders,
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      const message = await response.text();

      throw new Error(message || 'Failed to load registered events.');
    }

    const data: EventResponse[] = await response.json();

    setRegisteredEventIds(
      data.filter((event) => !event.isRegistrationCancelled).map((event) => event.eventId),
    );
    setCancelledRegistrationEventIds(
      data.filter((event) => event.isRegistrationCancelled).map((event) => event.eventId),
    );
  }
  // GET VENUES
  // GET /api/venues
  async function loadVenues() {
    const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/venues`);

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error('Failed to load venues.');
    }

    const data: Venue[] = await response.json();

    setVenues(data);
  }
  // GET ORGANIZATIONS
  // GET /api/organizations
  async function loadOrganizations() {
    // Managers need organization data

    if (!canManageEvents) {
      setOrganizations([]);
      return;
    }

    const authHeaders = await getAuthHeaders();

    const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/organizations`, {
      headers: authHeaders,
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error('Failed to load organizations.');
    }

    const data: Organization[] = await response.json();

    setOrganizations(data);
  }
  // GET EMPLOYEES
  // GET /api/employees
  async function loadEmployees() {
    if (!canManageEvents) {
      setEmployees([]);
      return;
    }

    const authHeaders = await getAuthHeaders();

    const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/employees`, {
      headers: authHeaders,
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error('Failed to load employees.');
    }

    const data: Employee[] = await response.json();

    setEmployees(data);
  }
  // REGISTER FOR EVENT
  // POST /api/events/{eventId}/register
  const registerForEvent = async (eventId: number) => {
    setError('');
    setSuccessMessage('');
    setRegisteringEventId(eventId);

    try {
      const authHeaders = await getAuthHeaders();

      const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events/${eventId}/register`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: authHeaders,
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();

        throw new Error(message || 'Failed to register for event.');
      }

      setRegisteredEventIds((previous) => [...previous.filter((id) => id !== eventId), eventId]);
      setCancelledRegistrationEventIds((previous) => previous.filter((id) => id !== eventId));

      setSuccessMessage('You have successfully registered for the event.');
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Event registration failed:', error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to register for event.');
    } finally {
      setRegisteringEventId(null);
    }
  };

  const cancelRegistration = async (eventId: number) => {
    setError('');
    setSuccessMessage('');
    setRegisteringEventId(eventId);

    try {
      const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events/${eventId}/register`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: await getAuthHeaders(),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();
        throw new Error(message || 'Failed to cancel your registration.');
      }

      setRegisteredEventIds((previous) => previous.filter((id) => id !== eventId));
      setCancelledRegistrationEventIds((previous) => [...new Set([...previous, eventId])]);
      setSuccessMessage('Your event registration has been cancelled.');
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Registration cancellation failed:', error);
      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to cancel your registration.');
    } finally {
      setRegisteringEventId(null);
    }
  };
  // CHECK WHETHER EVENT IS REGISTERED
  const isEventRegistered = (eventId: number) => {
    return registeredEventIds.includes(eventId);
  };

  const isRegistrationCancelled = (eventId: number) =>
    cancelledRegistrationEventIds.includes(eventId);
  // FORM INPUT CHANGE
  // RESET FORM
  const resetForm = () => {
    setFormData({
      eventName: '',
      startDate: '',
      endDate: '',
      venueId: '',
      budget: '0',
      eventType: '',
      orgId: '',
    });

    setEditingEventId(null);
    setShowCreateForm(false);

    if (routeEventId) {
      navigate('/events', { replace: true });
    }
  };
  // OPEN CREATE FORM
  const openCreateForm = () => {
    setError('');
    setSuccessMessage('');

    setEditingEventId(null);

    const start = new Date();
    start.setSeconds(0, 0);
    const end = new Date(start.getTime() + 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const toLocalInput = (date: Date) => {
      const localDate = new Date(date.getTime() - date.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
      return localDate.toISOString().slice(0, 16);
    };

    setFormData({
      eventName: '',
      startDate: toLocalInput(start),
      endDate: toLocalInput(end),
      venueId: '',
      budget: '0',
      eventType: '0',
      orgId: '',
    });

    setShowCreateForm(true);
  };

  // OPEN FORM FROM DASHBOARD

  useEffect(() => {
    if (searchParams.get('create') !== 'true') {
      return;
    }

    // Wait for account readiness
    if (!account) {
      return;
    }

    // Restrict creation to managers
    if (!canManageEvents) {
      setSearchParams({}, { replace: true });
      return;
    }

    const selectedDate = searchParams.get('date');

    // Use selected Dashboard date
    const validDate = selectedDate && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(selectedDate) ? selectedDate : '';

    setError('');
    setSuccessMessage('');
    setEditingEventId(null);

    const now = new Date();
    const startDate = validDate ? new Date(`${validDate}T09:00:00`) : now;
    if (
      startDate.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear() &&
      startDate.getMonth() === now.getMonth() &&
      startDate.getDate() === now.getDate()
    ) {
      startDate.setHours(now.getHours(), now.getMinutes(), 0, 0);
    }
    const endDate = new Date(startDate.getTime() + 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const toLocalInput = (date: Date) => {
      const adjustedDate = new Date(date.getTime() - date.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
      return adjustedDate.toISOString().slice(0, 16);
    };

    setFormData({
      eventName: '',
      startDate: toLocalInput(startDate),
      endDate: toLocalInput(endDate),
      venueId: '',
      budget: '0',
      eventType: '',
      orgId: '',
    });

    setShowCreateForm(true);

    // Clear query after opening
    setSearchParams({}, { replace: true });

    window.scrollTo({
      top: 0,
      behavior: 'smooth',
    });
  }, [searchParams, setSearchParams, account, accountId, canManageEvents]);
  // CONVERT API DATE TO DATETIME-LOCAL
  const convertToLocalDateTime = (date: string) => {
    const parsedDate = new Date(date);

    const year = parsedDate.getFullYear();

    const month = String(parsedDate.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');

    const day = String(parsedDate.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');

    const hours = String(parsedDate.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');

    const minutes = String(parsedDate.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');

    return `${year}-${month}-${day}T${hours}:${minutes}`;
  };
  // OPEN EDIT FORM
  const openEditForm = (event: EventResponse) => {
    setError('');
    setSuccessMessage('');

    setEditingEventId(event.eventId);

    setFormData({
      eventName: event.eventName,

      startDate: convertToLocalDateTime(event.startDate),

      endDate: convertToLocalDateTime(event.endDate),

      venueId: event.venueId?.toString() ?? '',

      budget: event.budget?.toString() ?? '',

      eventType: event.eventType.toString(),

      orgId: event.orgId?.toString() ?? '',
    });

    setShowCreateForm(true);

    window.scrollTo({
      top: 0,
      behavior: 'smooth',
    });
  };
  // OPEN AN EVENT FROM /events/:eventId
  // Load event into edit form
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!routeEventId || !account || !canManageEvents) {
      return;
    }

    const selectedEventId = Number(routeEventId);

    if (!Number.isInteger(selectedEventId) || selectedEventId <= 0) {
      setError('Invalid event ID.');
      return;
    }

    let cancelled = false;

    const loadEventForEditing = async () => {
      try {
        setError('');
        setSuccessMessage('');

        const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events/${selectedEventId}`, {
          headers: await getAuthHeaders(),
        });

        if (!response.ok) {
          const message = await response.text();
          throw new Error(message || 'Failed to load event details.');
        }

        const event: EventResponse = await response.json();

        if (!cancelled) {
          openEditForm(event);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Failed to open event for editing:', error);

        if (!cancelled) {
          setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to load event details.');
        }
      }
    };

    void loadEventForEditing();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
    // Reload when route identity changes
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [routeEventId, accountId, canManageEvents, getAccessToken]);
  // CREATE EVENT
  // POST /api/events
  const createEvent = async (values: EventFormData) => {
    const request = {
      eventName: values.eventName,

      startDate: new Date(values.startDate).toISOString(),

      endDate: new Date(values.endDate).toISOString(),

      venueId: Number(values.venueId),

      budget: Number(values.budget),

      eventType: Number(values.eventType),

      orgId: values.orgId === '' ? null : Number(values.orgId),
    };

    const authHeaders = await getAuthHeaders();

    const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...authHeaders,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(request),
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      const message = await response.text();

      throw new Error(message || 'Failed to create event.');
    }

    setSuccessMessage('Event created successfully.');

    await loadEvents();

    resetForm();
  };
  // UPDATE EVENT
  // PUT /api/events/{eventId}
  const updateEvent = async (values: EventFormData) => {
    if (editingEventId === null) {
      return;
    }

    const request = {
      eventName: values.eventName,

      startDate: new Date(values.startDate).toISOString(),

      endDate: new Date(values.endDate).toISOString(),

      budget: Number(values.budget),

      eventType: Number(values.eventType),

      venueId: values.venueId === '' ? null : Number(values.venueId),

      orgId: values.orgId === '' ? null : Number(values.orgId),
    };

    const authHeaders = await getAuthHeaders();

    const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events/${editingEventId}`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...authHeaders,
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(request),
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      const message = await response.text();

      throw new Error(message || 'Failed to update event.');
    }

    setSuccessMessage('Event updated successfully.');

    await loadEvents();

    resetForm();
  };
  // FORM SUBMIT
  const handleSubmit = async (values: EventFormData) => {
    if (saving) {
      return;
    }

    setError('');
    setSuccessMessage('');

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      if (editingEventId !== null) {
        await updateEvent(values);
      } else {
        await createEvent(values);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Event save failed:', error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to save event.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };
  // CANCEL EVENT
  // PATCH /api/events/{eventId}/cancel
  const cancelEvent = async (eventId: number) => {
    const confirmed = window.confirm('Are you sure you want to cancel this event?');

    if (!confirmed) {
      return;
    }

    setError('');
    setSuccessMessage('');

    try {
      const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events/${eventId}/cancel`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: await getAuthHeaders(),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();

        throw new Error(message || 'Failed to cancel event.');
      }

      setSuccessMessage('Event cancelled successfully.');

      await loadEvents();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Cancel event failed:', error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to cancel event.');
    }
  };
  // LOAD ASSIGNED EMPLOYEES FOR EVENT
  // GET /api/events/{eventId}/staff
  const loadAssignedStaff = async (eventId: number) => {
    const authHeaders = await getAuthHeaders();

    const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events/${eventId}/staff`, {
      headers: authHeaders,
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      const message = await response.text();

      throw new Error(message || 'Failed to load assigned employees.');
    }

    const data: StaffAssignment[] = await response.json();

    setAssignedStaff(data);
  };
  // OPEN ASSIGN TASK FORM
  const openAssignForm = async (event: EventResponse) => {
    setError('');
    setSuccessMessage('');

    setAssigningEvent(event);
    setSelectedEmployeeId('');
    setTask('');
    setDeadline('');
    setAssignedStaff([]);
    setShowAssignForm(true);

    try {
      await loadAssignedStaff(event.eventId);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to load assigned employees:', error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to load assigned employees.');
    }
  };
  // CLOSE ASSIGN TASK FORM
  const closeAssignForm = () => {
    setShowAssignForm(false);
    setAssigningEvent(null);
    setAssignedStaff([]);
    setSelectedEmployeeId('');
    setTask('');
    setDeadline('');
  };
  // ASSIGN EMPLOYEE TASK
  // POST /api/events/{eventId}/staff
  const assignEmployeeTask = async () => {
    if (!assigningEvent) {
      return;
    }

    const validationErrors: string[] = [];
    if (!selectedEmployeeId) {
      validationErrors.push('Please select an employee.');
    }

    if (!task.trim()) {
      validationErrors.push('Task is required.');
    }

    if (validationErrors.length > 0) {
      setError(validationErrors.join(' '));
      return;
    }

    setAssigning(true);
    setError('');
    setSuccessMessage('');

    try {
      const authHeaders = await getAuthHeaders();

      const request = {
        employeeId: Number(selectedEmployeeId),
        task: task.trim(),
        deadline: deadline ? new Date(deadline).toISOString() : null,
      };

      const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events/${assigningEvent.eventId}/staff`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          ...authHeaders,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(request),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();

        throw new Error(message || 'Failed to assign employee task.');
      }

      setSuccessMessage('Employee task assigned successfully.');

      setSelectedEmployeeId('');
      setTask('');
      setDeadline('');

      await loadAssignedStaff(assigningEvent.eventId);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Assign employee task failed:', error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to assign employee task.');
    } finally {
      setAssigning(false);
    }
  };

  const reassignDeclinedTask = async (
    assignment: Pick<StaffAssignment, 'task' | 'deadline'>,
    employeeId: number,
  ) => {
    if (!assigningEvent) return;

    setAssigning(true);
    setError('');
    setSuccessMessage('');
    try {
      const authHeaders = await getAuthHeaders();
      const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events/${assigningEvent.eventId}/staff`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...authHeaders },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          employeeId,
          task: assignment.task,
          deadline: assignment.deadline,
        }),
      });
      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();
        throw new Error(message || 'Failed to reassign the task.');
      }

      const employeeName = employees.find((employee) => employee.employeeId === employeeId)?.name;
      setSuccessMessage(`Task reassigned${employeeName ? ` to ${employeeName}` : ''}.`);
      await loadAssignedStaff(assigningEvent.eventId);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Failed to reassign employee task:', error);
      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to reassign the task.');
    } finally {
      setAssigning(false);
    }
  };
  // EVENT TYPE TEXT
  const getEventTypeText = (eventType: EventType) => {
    switch (eventType) {
      case 0:
        return 'Conference';

      case 1:
        return 'Workshop';

      case 2:
        return 'Webinar';

      case 3:
        return 'Cultural Fest';

      case 4:
        return 'Meeting';

      case 5:
        return 'Product Launch';

      default:
        return 'Unknown';
    }
  };
  // DATE DISPLAY
  const formatDate = (date: string) => {
    const parsedDate = new Date(date);

    return parsedDate.toLocaleString();
  };
  // STATUS SORT ORDER
  const getStatusSortOrder = (status: EventStatus) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 0:
        return 0;

      case 2:
        return 1;

      case 1:
        return 2;

      default:
        return 3;
    }
  };
  // FILTER EVENTS
  const filteredEvents = events
    .filter((event) => {
      const matchesSearch = event.eventName.toLowerCase().includes(searchText.toLowerCase());
      const now = Date.now();
      const start = new Date(event.startDate).getTime();
      const end = new Date(event.endDate).getTime();
      const displayStatus =
        event.status === 1
          ? 'cancelled'
          : event.status === 2 || end < now
            ? 'completed'
            : start <= now
              ? 'ongoing'
              : 'upcoming';
      const matchesStatus = statusFilter === 'all' || statusFilter === displayStatus;

      return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
    })
    .sort((firstEvent, secondEvent) => {
      const statusDifference =
        getStatusSortOrder(firstEvent.status) - getStatusSortOrder(secondEvent.status);

      if (statusDifference !== 0) {
        return statusDifference;
      }

      return new Date(firstEvent.startDate).getTime() - new Date(secondEvent.startDate).getTime();
    });


  // RENDER


  return (
    <div className="page-canvas min-h-full p-6 md:p-8">
      {/* PAGE HEADER */}
      <PageHeader
        title="Events"
        description={
          canManageEvents ? 'Create and manage your events.' : 'View and register for events.'
        }
        action={
          canManageEvents && (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              onClick={() => {
                if (showCreateForm) {
                  resetForm();
                } else {
                  openCreateForm();
                }
              }}
            >
              {showCreateForm ? 'Close' : '+ Create Event'}
            </button>
          )
        }
      />

      {/* ERROR MESSAGE */}

      {error && (
        <div className="mb-4 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CREATE / EDIT FORM */}
      {showCreateForm && canManageEvents && (
        <Modal
          open={showCreateForm}
          ariaLabel={editingEventId === null ? 'Create event' : 'Edit event'}
          onClose={resetForm}
          className="max-h-[92vh] w-full max-w-3xl overflow-y-auto rounded-xl bg-white shadow-2xl"
        >
          <EventEditorForm
            formData={formData}
            venues={venues}
            organizations={organizations}
            isEditing={editingEventId !== null}
            saving={saving}
            onSubmit={handleSubmit}
            onCancel={resetForm}
          />
        </Modal>
      )}

      {/* ASSIGN EMPLOYEE TASK FORM */}
      {showAssignForm && assigningEvent && canManageEvents && (
        <Modal
          open={showAssignForm}
          ariaLabel="Assign employee task"
          onClose={closeAssignForm}
          className="max-h-[92vh] w-full max-w-3xl overflow-y-auto rounded-xl bg-white shadow-2xl"
        >
          <EventTaskAssignmentPanel
            eventName={assigningEvent.eventName}
            employees={employees}
            assignedStaff={assignedStaff}
            selectedEmployeeId={selectedEmployeeId}
            setSelectedEmployeeId={setSelectedEmployeeId}
            task={task}
            setTask={setTask}
            deadline={deadline}
            setDeadline={setDeadline}
            assigning={assigning}
            onAssign={assignEmployeeTask}
            onReassign={reassignDeclinedTask}
          />
        </Modal>
      )}

      {/* EVENTS LIST */}
      <EventListPanel
        events={filteredEvents}
        loading={loading}
        searchValue={searchText}
        statusFilter={statusFilter}
        canManageEvents={canManageEvents}
        isAttendee={isAttendee}
        registeringEventId={registeringEventId}
        cancellingRegistrationEventId={registeringEventId}
        onSearchChange={setSearchText}
        onStatusFilterChange={setStatusFilter}
        isRegistered={isEventRegistered}
        isRegistrationCancelled={isRegistrationCancelled}
        getEventTypeText={getEventTypeText}
        formatDate={formatDate}
        onAssign={openAssignForm}
        onEdit={(id) => navigate(`/events/${id}`)}
        onCancel={cancelEvent}
        onRegister={registerForEvent}
        onCancelRegistration={cancelRegistration}
      />

      <FeedbackModal
        open={Boolean(error)}
        title="Action failed"
        message={error}
        tone="error"
        onClose={() => setError('')}
      />
      <FeedbackModal
        open={Boolean(successMessage)}
        title="Success"
        message={successMessage}
        onClose={() => setSuccessMessage('')}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

export default EventsPage;


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/events/schemas/eventFormSchema.ts
================================================================================
import { z } from 'zod';

export const eventFormSchema = z
  .object({
    eventName: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Event name is required.'),
    startDate: z.string().min(1, 'Start date is required.'),
    endDate: z.string().min(1, 'End date is required.'),
    venueId: z.string().min(1, 'Please select a venue.'),
    budget: z
      .string()
      .min(1, 'Budget is required.')
      .refine((value) => Number.isFinite(Number(value)) && Number(value) >= 0, {
        message: 'Budget must be zero or greater.',
      }),
    eventType: z.string().refine((value) => ['0', '1', '2', '3', '4', '5'].includes(value), {
      message: 'Please select an event type.',
    }),
    orgId: z.string(),
  })
  .refine((values) => new Date(values.endDate) > new Date(values.startDate), {
    path: ['endDate'],
    message: 'End date must be after the start date.',
  });

export type EventFormData = z.infer<typeof eventFormSchema>;


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/participants/index.ts

export { default } from './pages/ParticipantsPage';


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/participants/pages/ParticipantsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { apiRequest } from '../../../shared/api/apiClient';
import { useAccessToken } from '@/shared/hooks/useAccessToken';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

import SearchBar from '@/shared/components/SearchBar';
import EventFilter from '@/shared/components/EventFilter';
import PageHeader from '@/shared/components/PageHeader';
import FormField from '@/shared/components/FormField';
import Modal from '@/shared/components/Modal';

interface Attendee {
  attendeeId: number;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  ticket: string;
}

interface EventItem {
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
}

interface Participant {
  attendeeId: number;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
  registeredAt: string;
}

interface AttendeeForm {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  ticket: string;
  eventId: string;
}

const API_URL = '/api';

function ParticipantsPage() {
  const { account, getAccessToken } = useAccessToken();

  const [selectedEvent, setSelectedEvent] = useState('all');

  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');

  const [participants, setParticipants] = useState<Participant[]>([]);

  const [events, setEvents] = useState<EventItem[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const [showAddForm, setShowAddForm] = useState(false);

  const [attendeeForm, setAttendeeForm] = useState<AttendeeForm>({
    name: '',
    email: '',
    phone: '',
    ticket: '',
    eventId: '',
  });



  //Load events and participants.
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    const loadData = async () => {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError('');

        //Check Microsoft account.
        if (!account) {
          throw new Error('Please log in with Microsoft first.');
        }

        //Get Microsoft access token.
        const token = await getAccessToken();

        if (cancelled) {
          return;
        }

        const headers = {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        };

        //STEP 1: Load events
        //GET /api/events

        const eventsResponse = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events`, {
          method: 'GET',
          headers,
        });

        if (!eventsResponse.ok) {
          throw new Error(`Failed to load events. Status: ${eventsResponse.status}`);
        }

        const eventData: EventItem[] = await eventsResponse.json();

        if (cancelled) {
          return;
        }

        setEvents(eventData);

        //STEP 2: Load attendees
        //GET /api/attendees

        const attendeesResponse = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/attendees`, {
          method: 'GET',
          headers,
        });

        if (!attendeesResponse.ok) {
          throw new Error(`Failed to load attendees. Status: ${attendeesResponse.status}`);
        }

        const attendeeData: Attendee[] = await attendeesResponse.json();
        void attendeeData;

        if (cancelled) {
          return;
        }

        //STEP 3: Load event attendees

        const allParticipants: Participant[] = [];

        for (const event of eventData) {
          if (cancelled) {
            return;
          }

          const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events/${event.eventId}/attendees`, {
            method: 'GET',
            headers,
          });

          //Continue after event failures.
          if (!response.ok) {
            console.error(
              `Failed to load attendees for event ${event.eventId}. Status: ${response.status}`,
            );

            continue;
          }

          const eventAttendeeData: Attendee[] = await response.json();

          //Convert attendee response data.
          eventAttendeeData.forEach((attendee) => {
            allParticipants.push({
              attendeeId: attendee.attendeeId,

              name: attendee.name,

              email: attendee.email,

              eventId: event.eventId,

              eventName: event.eventName,

              registeredAt: 'Registered',
            });
          });
        }

        if (cancelled) {
          return;
        }

        //Save participants in React state.
        setParticipants(allParticipants);
      } catch (error) {
        if (cancelled) {
          return;
        }

        console.error('Error loading participants:', error);

        setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unable to load participants.');
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    };

    loadData();

    //Cancel active request on unmount.
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };

    //Reload when account changes.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [account?.homeAccountId]);

  //Filter participants by search.
  const filteredParticipants = participants.filter((participant) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      participant.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
      participant.email.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase());

    const matchesEvent =
      selectedEvent === 'all' || participant.eventId.toString() === selectedEvent;

    return matchesSearch && matchesEvent;
  });

  //Open participant form.
  const openAddParticipant = () => {
    setAttendeeForm({
      name: '',
      email: '',
      phone: '',
      ticket: '',
      eventId: '',
    });

    setError('');

    setShowAddForm(true);
  };

  //Handle participant form changes.
  const handleAttendeeChange = (
    event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement> | React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>,
  ) => {
    const { name, value } = event.target;

    setAttendeeForm((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  };

  //Register attendee for selected event.
  const addParticipant = async (event: React.FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault();

    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError('');

      if (!attendeeForm.eventId) {
        throw new Error('Please select an event.');
      }

      //Get Microsoft access token.
      const token = await getAccessToken();

      const headers = {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',

        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      };

      //STEP 1: Create attendee
      //POST /api/attendees
      const attendeeResponse = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/attendees`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers,

        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: attendeeForm.name,
          email: attendeeForm.email,
          phone: attendeeForm.phone,
          ticket: attendeeForm.ticket,
        }),
      });

      if (!attendeeResponse.ok) {
        const message = await attendeeResponse.text();

        throw new Error(message || `Failed to create attendee. Status: ${attendeeResponse.status}`);
      }

      const newAttendee: Attendee = await attendeeResponse.json();

      //STEP 2: Register attendee
      //POST /api/events/{eventId}/attendees/{attendeeId}
      const registrationResponse = await apiRequest(
        `${API_URL}/events/${attendeeForm.eventId}/attendees/${newAttendee.attendeeId}`,
        {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
        },
      );

      if (!registrationResponse.ok) {
        const message = await registrationResponse.text();

        throw new Error(
          message ||
            `Attendee was created, but registration failed. Status: ${registrationResponse.status}`,
        );
      }

      //Find selected event.
      const selectedEventData = events.find(
        (item) => item.eventId.toString() === attendeeForm.eventId,
      );

      //Add participant to state.
      if (selectedEventData) {
        setParticipants((previous) => [
          ...previous,
          {
            attendeeId: newAttendee.attendeeId,

            name: newAttendee.name,

            email: newAttendee.email,

            eventId: selectedEventData.eventId,

            eventName: selectedEventData.eventName,

            registeredAt: 'Registered',
          },
        ]);
      }

      //Close participant form.
      setShowAddForm(false);

      setAttendeeForm({
        name: '',
        email: '',
        phone: '',
        ticket: '',
        eventId: '',
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error adding participant:', error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unable to add participant.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  //Remove event registration.
  const removeParticipant = async (eventId: number, attendeeId: number) => {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      'Are you sure you want to remove this participant from the event?',
    );

    if (!confirmed) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      setError('');

      //Get Microsoft access token.
      const token = await getAccessToken();

      //DELETE event registration
      //DELETE /api/events/{eventId}/attendees/{attendeeId}
      const response = await apiRequest(`${API_URL}/events/${eventId}/attendees/${attendeeId}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',

        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = await response.text();

        throw new Error(message || `Failed to remove participant. Status: ${response.status}`);
      }

      //Remove participant from state.
      setParticipants((previous) =>
        previous.filter(
          (participant) =>
            !(participant.eventId === eventId && participant.attendeeId === attendeeId),
        ),
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error removing participant:', error);

      setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unable to remove participant.');
    }
  };

  //Tailwind style classes.

  const inputClass =
    'w-full rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-text-primary outline-none transition focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-primary/20';

  const primaryButtonClass =
    'rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50';

  const secondaryButtonClass =
    'rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary transition hover:border-brand-primary hover:bg-surface-blue hover:text-brand-primary-hover';

  return (
    <div
      className="
        min-h-full
        page-canvas
        p-6
        md:p-8
      "
    >
      {/* PAGE HEADER */}
      <PageHeader
        title="Participants"
        description="Manage attendees and event registrations."
        action={
          <button
            type="button"
            className="rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-brand-primary-hover"
            onClick={openAddParticipant}
          >
            + Add Participant
          </button>
        }
      />

      {/* MAIN CONTENT CARD */}

      <div className="glass-panel w-full overflow-hidden">
        {/* ERROR MESSAGE */}

        {error && !showAddForm && (
          <div
            className="
              mx-6
              mt-6
              rounded-lg
              border
              border-red-200
              bg-red-50
              px-4
              py-3
              text-sm
              text-red-700
            "
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ADD PARTICIPANT FORM */}

        <Modal
          open={showAddForm}
          ariaLabel="Add participant"
          onClose={() => setShowAddForm(false)}
          className="max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-2xl overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-border-accent/40 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl"
        >
          <form onSubmit={addParticipant}>
            <h3
              className="
                mb-5
                text-lg
                font-semibold
                text-text-primary
              "
            >
              Add Participant
            </h3>

            {error && (
              <p role="alert" className="mb-4 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
                {error}
              </p>
            )}

            <div
              className="
                grid
                grid-cols-1
                gap-5
                md:grid-cols-2
              "
            >
              {/* NAME */}

              <FormField label="Name" className="gap-2" required>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  name="name"
                  value={attendeeForm.name}
                  onChange={handleAttendeeChange}
                  className={inputClass}
                  required
                />
              </FormField>

              {/* EMAIL */}

              <FormField label="Email" className="gap-2" required>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  name="email"
                  value={attendeeForm.email}
                  onChange={handleAttendeeChange}
                  className={inputClass}
                  required
                />
              </FormField>

              {/* PHONE */}

              <FormField label="Phone" className="gap-2" required>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  name="phone"
                  value={attendeeForm.phone}
                  onChange={handleAttendeeChange}
                  className={inputClass}
                  required
                />
              </FormField>

              {/* TICKET */}

              <FormField label="Ticket" className="gap-2" required>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  name="ticket"
                  value={attendeeForm.ticket}
                  onChange={handleAttendeeChange}
                  className={inputClass}
                  required
                />
              </FormField>

              {/* EVENT */}

              <FormField label="Event" className="gap-2" required>
                <select
                  name="eventId"
                  value={attendeeForm.eventId}
                  onChange={handleAttendeeChange}
                  className={inputClass}
                  required
                >
                  <option value="">Select event</option>

                  {events.map((event) => (
                    <option key={event.eventId} value={event.eventId}>
                      {event.eventName}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </FormField>
            </div>

            {/* FORM ACTIONS */}

            <div
              className="
                mt-6
                flex
                flex-wrap
                justify-end
                gap-3
              "
            >

              <button type="submit" className={primaryButtonClass} disabled={loading}>
                {loading ? 'Adding...' : 'Add Participant'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>

        {/* FILTERS */}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 border-b border-border-subtle p-6 md:grid-cols-2">
          <SearchBar value={searchTerm} onChange={setSearchTerm} fullWidth />
          <EventFilter value={selectedEvent} events={events} onChange={setSelectedEvent} />
        </div>
        {/* TABLE */}

        <div
          className="
            w-full
            overflow-x-auto
          "
        >
          <table
            className="
              table-grid
              min-w-full
              border-collapse
              text-left
              text-sm
            "
          >
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th
                  className="
                    whitespace-nowrap
                    bg-surface-subtle
                    px-5
                    py-3
                    text-xs
                    font-semibold
                    uppercase
                    tracking-wide
                    text-text-muted
                  "
                >
                  Attendee
                </th>

                <th
                  className="
                    whitespace-nowrap
                    bg-surface-subtle
                    px-5
                    py-3
                    text-xs
                    font-semibold
                    uppercase
                    tracking-wide
                    text-text-muted
                  "
                >
                  Email
                </th>

                <th
                  className="
                    whitespace-nowrap
                    bg-surface-subtle
                    px-5
                    py-3
                    text-xs
                    font-semibold
                    uppercase
                    tracking-wide
                    text-text-muted
                  "
                >
                  Event
                </th>

                <th
                  className="
                    whitespace-nowrap
                    bg-surface-subtle
                    px-5
                    py-3
                    text-xs
                    font-semibold
                    uppercase
                    tracking-wide
                    text-text-muted
                  "
                >
                  Registration
                </th>

                <th
                  className="
                    whitespace-nowrap
                    bg-surface-subtle
                    px-5
                    py-3
                    text-xs
                    font-semibold
                    uppercase
                    tracking-wide
                    text-text-muted
                  "
                >
                  Actions
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {/* LOADING */}

              {loading ? (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan={5}
                    className="
                      border-t
                      border-border-subtle
                    "
                  >
                    <div
                      className="
                        flex
                        flex-col
                        items-center
                        justify-center
                        px-6
                        py-12
                        text-center
                      "
                    >
                      <h3
                        className="
                          text-base
                          font-semibold
                          text-text-primary
                        "
                      >
                        Loading participants...
                      </h3>

                      <p
                        className="
                          mt-2
                          text-sm
                          text-text-muted
                        "
                      >
                        Please wait while participants are loaded.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : error ? (
                /* ERROR */

                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan={5}
                    className="
                      border-t
                      border-border-subtle
                    "
                  >
                    <div
                      className="
                        flex
                        flex-col
                        items-center
                        justify-center
                        px-6
                        py-12
                        text-center
                      "
                    >
                      <h3
                        className="
                          text-base
                          font-semibold
                          text-red-600
                        "
                      >
                        {error}
                      </h3>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : filteredParticipants.length === 0 ? (
                /* EMPTY */

                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan={5}
                    className="
                      border-t
                      border-border-subtle
                    "
                  >
                    <div
                      className="
                        flex
                        flex-col
                        items-center
                        justify-center
                        px-6
                        py-12
                        text-center
                      "
                    >
                      <h3
                        className="
                          text-base
                          font-semibold
                          text-text-primary
                        "
                      >
                        No participants found
                      </h3>

                      <p
                        className="
                          mt-2
                          text-sm
                          text-text-muted
                        "
                      >
                        Registered attendees will appear here.
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                /* PARTICIPANTS */

                filteredParticipants.map((participant) => (
                  <tr
                    key={`${participant.eventId}-${participant.attendeeId}`}
                    className="
                        transition
                        hover:bg-surface-muted
                      "
                  >
                    <td
                      className="
                          border-t
                          border-border-subtle
                          px-5
                          py-4
                          text-sm
                          font-medium
                          text-text-primary
                        "
                    >
                      {participant.name}
                    </td>

                    <td
                      className="
                          border-t
                          border-border-subtle
                          px-5
                          py-4
                          text-sm
                          text-text-secondary
                        "
                    >
                      {participant.email}
                    </td>

                    <td
                      className="
                          border-t
                          border-border-subtle
                          px-5
                          py-4
                          text-sm
                          text-text-secondary
                        "
                    >
                      {participant.eventName}
                    </td>

                    <td
                      className="
                          border-t
                          border-border-subtle
                          px-5
                          py-4
                          text-sm
                          text-text-muted
                        "
                    >
                      {participant.registeredAt}
                    </td>

                    <td
                      className="
                          border-t
                          border-border-subtle
                          px-5
                          py-4
                        "
                    >
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        aria-label="Remove participant"
                        title="Remove participant"
                        className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-lg border border-surface-error bg-white p-0 text-state-error transition hover:bg-surface-error"
                        onClick={() =>
                          removeParticipant(participant.eventId, participant.attendeeId)
                        }
                      >
                        <svg
                          aria-hidden="true"
                          className="h-4 w-4"
                          fill="none"
                          stroke="currentColor"
                          strokeLinecap="round"
                          strokeLinejoin="round"
                          strokeWidth="1.8"
                          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                        >
                          <path d="M3 6h18M8 6V4h8v2m2 0-1 14H7L6 6m4 4v6m4-6v6" />
                        </svg>
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default ParticipantsPage;

================================================================================
FILE: src/features/tasks/pages/MyTaskPageAngular.tsx
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { env } from '@/config/env';
import { useAccessToken } from '@/shared/hooks/useAccessToken';

const angularAssetBase = '/angular/my-tasks/browser';
const angularAssetVersion = 'task-actions-aligned-v4';
const elementName = 'my-tasks-angular';
let angularElementLoad: Promise<void> | undefined;

interface MyTasksAngularElement extends HTMLElement {
  apiBaseUrl: string;
  tokenProvider: () => Promise<string>;
}

function loadStylesheet(): void {
  const href = `${angularAssetBase}/styles.css?v=${angularAssetVersion}`;
  if (document.querySelector(`link[href="${href}"]`)) return;

  const stylesheet = document.createElement('link');
  stylesheet.rel = 'stylesheet';
  stylesheet.href = href;
  document.head.append(stylesheet);
}

function loadModule(src: string): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.type = 'module';
    script.src = src;
    script.onload = () => resolve();
    script.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Unable to load the Angular My Tasks page.'));
    document.head.append(script);
  });
}

function ensureAngularElement(): Promise<void> {
  if (customElements.get(elementName)) return Promise.resolve();
  if (angularElementLoad) return angularElementLoad;

  loadStylesheet();
  angularElementLoad = loadModule(`${angularAssetBase}/polyfills.js?v=${angularAssetVersion}`)
    .then(() => loadModule(`${angularAssetBase}/main.js?v=${angularAssetVersion}`))
    .then(() => customElements.whenDefined(elementName))
    .then(() => undefined)
    .catch((error: unknown) => {
      angularElementLoad = undefined;
      throw error;
    });

  return angularElementLoad;
}

function MyTaskPageAngular() {
  const { getAccessToken } = useAccessToken('Please sign in before loading your tasks.');
  const hostRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const tokenProviderRef = useRef<() => Promise<string>>(async () => {
    throw new Error('Authentication is not ready.');
  });
  const [elementReady, setElementReady] = useState(false);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState('');


  useEffect(() => {
    tokenProviderRef.current = getAccessToken;
  }, [getAccessToken]);

  const provideAccessToken = useCallback(() => tokenProviderRef.current(), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    void ensureAngularElement()
      .then(() => {
        if (active) setElementReady(true);
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => {
        console.error('Failed to initialize Angular My Tasks:', error);
        if (active) setLoadError('Unable to load My Tasks. Please refresh the page.');
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!elementReady || !hostRef.current) return;

    const element = document.createElement(elementName) as MyTasksAngularElement;
    element.apiBaseUrl = env.VITE_API_BASE_URL ?? '';
    element.tokenProvider = provideAccessToken;
    hostRef.current.replaceChildren(element);

    return () => {
      element.remove();
    };
  }, [elementReady, provideAccessToken]);

  return (
    <div className="page-canvas min-h-[calc(100vh-73px)]">
      {loadError ? (
        <p className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-6 py-10 text-sm text-state-error" role="alert">
          {loadError}
        </p>
      ) : (
        <div ref={hostRef} aria-busy={!elementReady}>
          {!elementReady && (
            <p className="px-6 py-10 text-center text-sm text-text-muted" role="status">
              Loading My Tasks...
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default MyTaskPageAngular;

FILE: src/shared/api/apiClient.ts

import { env } from '../../config/env';

export async function apiRequest(input: string | URL, init?: RequestInit): Promise<Response> {
  const path = String(input);
  const url = path.startsWith('http') ? path : `${env.VITE_API_BASE_URL ?? ''}${path}`;
  const response = await fetch(url, init);

  return response;
}
