interface EventItem {
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
}

interface EventFilterProps {
  value: string;
  events: EventItem[];
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
}

function EventFilter({ value, events, onChange }: EventFilterProps) {
  return (
    <select
      value={value}
      onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
      className="w-full rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-text-primary outline-none transition focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-primary/20"
    >
      <option value="all">All Events</option>

      {events.map((event) => (
        <option key={event.eventId} value={event.eventId}>
          {event.eventName}
        </option>
      ))}
    </select>
  );
}

export default EventFilter;


================================================================================
FILE: src/shared/components/EventNotifications.tsx
================================================================================
import { apiRequest } from '../api/apiClient';
import { useAccessToken } from '@/shared/hooks/useAccessToken';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

type EventStatus = 'Scheduled' | 'Cancelled' | 'Completed';

interface EventDto {
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
  startDate: string;
  endDate: string;
  status: EventStatus | number;
  isRegistrationCancelled?: boolean;
}

interface EmployeeTaskDto {
  assignmentId: number;
  eventId: number;
  eventName: string;
  employeeId: number;
  name: string;
  task: string;
  deadline: string | null;
  status: string;
}
interface NotificationItem {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  startDate: string;
  createdAt: number;
  read: boolean;
}

const REMINDER_WINDOW_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;

function getEventStatus(status: EventStatus | number): EventStatus {
  if (typeof status === 'number') {
    if (status === 1) return 'Cancelled';
    if (status === 2) return 'Completed';
    return 'Scheduled';
  }

  return status;
}

function formatEventTime(date: string) {
  return new Date(date).toLocaleString([], {
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    hour: 'numeric',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });
}

function EventNotifications() {
  const { account, getAccessToken } = useAccessToken();
  const accountId = account?.homeAccountId;
  const roles = (account?.idTokenClaims?.roles as string[]) ?? [];
  const isAttendee = roles.includes('Attendee');
  const isAdmin = roles.includes('Admin');
  const isEmployee = roles.includes('Employee') || roles.includes('Admin');

  const [events, setEvents] = useState<EventDto[]>([]);
  const [tasks, setTasks] = useState<EmployeeTaskDto[]>([]);
  const [adminTasks, setAdminTasks] = useState<EmployeeTaskDto[]>([]);
  const [adminTasksLoaded, setAdminTasksLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<NotificationItem[]>([]);
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const notifiedEventIds = useRef<Set<number>>(new Set());
  const notifiedTaskReminders = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const adminTaskStatuses = useRef<Map<number, string> | null>(null);
  const notifiedAdminOverdue = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const eventIdsKey = events.map((event) => event.eventId).join(',');

  // Fetch the events visible to the current user.
  useEffect(() => {
    const activeAccount = account;
    if (!activeAccount) {
      setEvents([]);
      return;
    }

    let isMounted = true;

    const loadEvents = async () => {
      try {
        let response: Response;

        if (isAttendee) {
          const accessToken = await getAccessToken();

          response = await apiRequest('/api/events/my-registrations', {
            headers: {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
            },
          });
        } else {
          response = await apiRequest('/api/events');
        }

        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(`Unable to load events (${response.status}).`);
        }

        const data: EventDto[] = await response.json();

        if (isMounted) {
          setEvents(data);
          setError('');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Unable to load notification events:', err);

        if (isMounted) {
          setError('Could not refresh event reminders.');
        }
      }
    };

    void loadEvents();

    // Refresh in case an event is added, changed, or cancelled.
    const refreshId = window.setInterval(() => {
      void loadEvents();
    }, 60_000);

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      window.clearInterval(refreshId);
    };
  }, [accountId, getAccessToken, isAttendee]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const activeAccount = account;
    if (!activeAccount || !isEmployee) {
      setTasks([]);
      return;
    }

    let isMounted = true;

    const loadTasks = async () => {
      try {
        const accessToken = await getAccessToken();
        const response = await apiRequest('/api/employees/my-tasks', {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` },
        });

        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(`Unable to load assigned tasks (${response.status}).`);
        }

        const data: EmployeeTaskDto[] = await response.json();
        if (isMounted) setTasks(data);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Unable to load task reminders:', err);
      }
    };

    void loadTasks();
    const refreshId = window.setInterval(() => void loadTasks(), 60_000);

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      window.clearInterval(refreshId);
    };
  }, [accountId, getAccessToken, isEmployee]);

  // Admins monitor task assignments across every event. 
  // Employees continue to receive reminders only for their own assigned tasks above.
  useEffect(() => {
    const activeAccount = account;
    if (!activeAccount || !isAdmin) {
      setAdminTasks([]);
      setAdminTasksLoaded(false);
      adminTaskStatuses.current = null;
      return;
    }

    const eventIds = eventIdsKey ? eventIdsKey.split(',').map(Number) : [];

    // Wait until the event list is available before baselining task statuses.
    if (eventIds.length === 0) return;

    let isMounted = true;
    let isLoading = false;

    const loadAdminTasks = async () => {
      if (isLoading) return;
      isLoading = true;

      try {
        const accessToken = await getAccessToken();
        const headers = {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
        };
        const eventAssignments = await Promise.all(
          eventIds.map(async (eventId) => {
            const response = await apiRequest(`/api/events/${eventId}/staff`, {
              headers,
            });

            if (!response.ok) {
              throw new Error(`Unable to load event task assignments (${response.status}).`);
            }

            return (await response.json()) as EmployeeTaskDto[];
          }),
        );

        if (isMounted) {
          setAdminTasks(eventAssignments.flat());
          setAdminTasksLoaded(true);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Unable to load admin task notifications:', err);
      } finally {
        isLoading = false;
      }
    };

    void loadAdminTasks();
    const refreshId = window.setInterval(() => void loadAdminTasks(), 60_000);

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
      window.clearInterval(refreshId);
    };
  }, [accountId, eventIdsKey, getAccessToken, isAdmin]);

  // Notify admins when an assignment changes from incomplete to completed.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAdmin || !adminTasksLoaded) return;

    const previousStatuses = adminTaskStatuses.current;
    const currentStatuses = new Map(
      adminTasks.map((task) => [task.assignmentId, task.status.toLowerCase()]),
    );

    if (previousStatuses) {
      const now = Date.now();

      for (const task of adminTasks) {
        const wasCompleted = previousStatuses.get(task.assignmentId) === 'completed';
        const isCompleted = task.status.toLowerCase() === 'completed';

        if (!wasCompleted && isCompleted) {
          setNotifications((current) => [
            {
              id: task.assignmentId,
              title: 'Task completed',
              message: `${task.name || `Employee #${task.employeeId}`} completed "${task.task}" for ${task.eventName}.`,
              startDate: new Date(now).toISOString(),
              createdAt: now,
              read: false,
            },
            ...current,
          ]);
        }
      }
    }

    adminTaskStatuses.current = currentStatuses;
  }, [adminTasks, adminTasksLoaded, isAdmin]);

  // Admin overdue alerts identify the employee and the task that missed its
  // deadline. These alerts are separate from employees' personal reminders.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isAdmin || !adminTasksLoaded) return;

    const now = Date.now();

    for (const task of adminTasks) {
      if (!task.deadline || task.status.toLowerCase() === 'completed') continue;

      const deadline = new Date(task.deadline).getTime();
      if (!Number.isFinite(deadline) || deadline > now) continue;

      const overdueKey = `${task.assignmentId}:${task.deadline}:admin-overdue`;
      if (notifiedAdminOverdue.current.has(overdueKey)) continue;

      notifiedAdminOverdue.current.add(overdueKey);
      setNotifications((current) => [
        {
          id: task.assignmentId,
          title: 'Employee task overdue',
          message: `${task.name || `Employee #${task.employeeId}`} is past due on "${task.task}" for ${task.eventName}. Deadline: ${formatEventTime(task.deadline!)}.`,
          startDate: task.deadline!,
          createdAt: now,
          read: false,
        },
        ...current,
      ]);
    }
  }, [adminTasks, adminTasksLoaded, isAdmin]);

  // Check upcoming events frequently so reminders appear near the
  // 15-minute mark, including when the app opens within that window.
  useEffect(() => {
    const checkUpcomingEvents = () => {
      const now = Date.now();

      for (const event of events) {
        if (getEventStatus(event.status) === 'Cancelled' || event.isRegistrationCancelled) continue;
        if (notifiedEventIds.current.has(event.eventId)) continue;

        const startTime = new Date(event.startDate).getTime();

        if (!Number.isFinite(startTime) || startTime <= now) continue;

        const timeUntilStart = startTime - now;

        if (timeUntilStart <= REMINDER_WINDOW_MS) {
          notifiedEventIds.current.add(event.eventId);

          setNotifications((current) => [
            {
              id: event.eventId,
              title: 'Event starting soon',
              message: `${event.eventName} starts at ${formatEventTime(event.startDate)}.`,
              startDate: event.startDate,
              createdAt: now,
              read: false,
            },
            ...current,
          ]);
        }
      }
    };

    checkUpcomingEvents();

    const checkId = window.setInterval(checkUpcomingEvents, 15_000);

    return () => window.clearInterval(checkId);
  }, [events]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const checkTaskDeadlines = () => {
      const now = Date.now();
      const reminderStages = [
        {
          key: '24-hours',
          threshold: 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
          lowerBound: 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
          timeLeft: '24 hours',
        },
        {
          key: '2-hours',
          threshold: 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
          lowerBound: 30 * 60 * 1000,
          timeLeft: '2 hours',
        },
        {
          key: '30-minutes',
          threshold: 30 * 60 * 1000,
          lowerBound: 0,
          timeLeft: '0.5 hours (30 minutes)',
        },
      ];

      for (const task of tasks) {
        if (!task.deadline || task.status.toLowerCase() === 'completed') continue;

        const deadline = new Date(task.deadline).getTime();
        const timeRemaining = deadline - now;
        if (!Number.isFinite(deadline)) continue;

        if (timeRemaining <= 0) {
          const overdueKey = `${task.assignmentId}:${task.employeeId}:${task.task}:${task.deadline}:overdue`;
          if (notifiedTaskReminders.current.has(overdueKey)) continue;

          notifiedTaskReminders.current.add(overdueKey);
          setNotifications((current) => [
            {
              id: task.assignmentId,
              title: 'Task overdue',
              message: `${task.eventName} | ${task.task}: the deadline has passed and this task is still incomplete.`,
              startDate: task.deadline!,
              createdAt: now,
              read: false,
            },
            ...current,
          ]);
          continue;
        }

        const stage = reminderStages.find(
          (reminder) => timeRemaining <= reminder.threshold && timeRemaining > reminder.lowerBound,
        );
        if (!stage) continue;

        const reminderKey = `${task.assignmentId}:${task.employeeId}:${task.task}:${task.deadline}:${stage.key}`;
        if (notifiedTaskReminders.current.has(reminderKey)) continue;

        notifiedTaskReminders.current.add(reminderKey);
        setNotifications((current) => [
          {
            id: task.eventId,
            title: 'Task deadline reminder',
            message: `${task.eventName} | ${task.task}: ${stage.timeLeft} left to complete.`,
            startDate: task.deadline ?? '',
            createdAt: now,
            read: false,
          },
          ...current,
        ]);
      }
    };

    checkTaskDeadlines();
    const checkId = window.setInterval(checkTaskDeadlines, 15_000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(checkId);
  }, [tasks]);

  // Close the notification panel when clicking outside it.
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleOutsideClick = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setIsOpen(false);
      }
    };

    const handleEscape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') setIsOpen(false);
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleOutsideClick);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleOutsideClick);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);
    };
  }, []);

  const unreadCount = notifications.filter((item) => !item.read).length;

  const toggleNotifications = () => {
    setIsOpen((open) => !open);
    setNotifications((current) => current.map((item) => ({ ...item, read: true })));
  };

  const clearNotifications = () => {
    setNotifications([]);
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={menuRef}>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={toggleNotifications}
        aria-label={unreadCount > 0 ? `Notifications, ${unreadCount} unread` : 'Notifications'}
        aria-expanded={isOpen}
        className="relative flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-gray-600 transition hover:bg-gray-100 hover:text-blue-600 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
      >
        <svg
          className="h-6 w-6"
          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          fill="none"
          stroke="currentColor"
          strokeWidth="1.8"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          <path
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            d="M18 8a6 6 0 00-12 0c0 7-3 7-3 9h18c0-2-3-2-3-9M10 21h4"
          />
        </svg>

        {unreadCount > 0 && (
          <span className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 flex h-5 min-w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-red-600 px-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-white ring-2 ring-white">
            {unreadCount > 9 ? '9+' : unreadCount}
          </span>
        )}
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div className="absolute right-0 z-50 mt-3 w-[min(360px,calc(100vw-2rem))] overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border-accent/35 bg-white shadow-popover">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-gray-100 px-4 py-3">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Notifications</h2>
              <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-gray-500">Event and task reminders</p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {notifications.length > 0 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={clearNotifications}
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
                >
                  Clear all
                </button>
              )}
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Close notifications"
                onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                className="grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-full text-xl leading-none text-gray-600 transition hover:bg-gray-100 hover:text-gray-900"
              >
                &times;
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <p className="border-b border-amber-100 bg-amber-50 px-4 py-2 text-xs text-amber-800">
              {error}
            </p>
          )}

          <div className="max-h-96 overflow-y-auto">
            {notifications.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="px-5 py-8 text-center">
                <div className="mx-auto flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                  <svg
                    className="h-5 w-5"
                    viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                    fill="none"
                    stroke="currentColor"
                    strokeWidth="1.8"
                    aria-hidden="true"
                  >
                    <path
                      strokeLinecap="round"
                      strokeLinejoin="round"
                      d="M12 8v4l2.5 2.5M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"
                    />
                  </svg>
                </div>
                <p className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">You're all caught up</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
                  We'll remind you before events and assigned task deadlines.
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              notifications.map((item) => (
                <div
                  key={`${item.id}-${item.createdAt}`}
                  className="flex gap-3 border-b border-gray-100 px-4 py-4 last:border-b-0 hover:bg-gray-50"
                >
                  <span className="mt-0.5 flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-orange-50 text-orange-600">
                    <svg
                      className="h-5 w-5"
                      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                      fill="none"
                      stroke="currentColor"
                      strokeWidth="1.8"
                      aria-hidden="true"
                    >
                      <path
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                        strokeLinejoin="round"
                        d="M12 8v4l2.5 2.5M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"
                      />
                    </svg>
                  </span>

                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                      <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900">{item.title}</p>
                      {!item.read && (
                        <span className="mt-1.5 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-blue-600" />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-1 break-words text-sm text-gray-600">{item.message}</p>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-400">{formatEventTime(item.startDate)}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default EventNotifications;

FILE: src/shared/components/FormField.tsx
================================================================================
import { cloneElement, isValidElement, useId, type ReactElement, type ReactNode } from 'react';

interface FormFieldProps {
  label: string;
  htmlFor?: string;
  className?: string;
  required?: boolean;
  children: ReactNode;
}

function FormField({ label, htmlFor, className = '', required = false, children }: FormFieldProps) {
  const generatedId = useId();
  const controlId = htmlFor ?? generatedId;
  const control = isValidElement(children)
    ? cloneElement(children as ReactElement<{ id?: string }>, {
        id: (children as ReactElement<{ id?: string }>).props.id ?? controlId,
      })
    : children;

  return (
    <div className={`grid gap-1 text-sm font-medium ${className}`}>
      <label htmlFor={controlId}>
        {label}
        {required && <span aria-hidden="true" className="ml-1 text-red-600">*</span>}
      </label>
      {control}
    </div>
  );
}

export default FormField;

FILE: src/shared/components/Login.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useMsal } from '@azure/msal-react';
import { InteractionStatus, type RedirectRequest } from '@azure/msal-browser';

const loginRequest: RedirectRequest = {
  scopes: ['User.Read'],
};

function Login() {
  const { instance, accounts, inProgress } = useMsal();

  const navigate = useNavigate();

  useEffect(() => {
    const account = accounts[0];
    if (account) {
      instance.setActiveAccount(account);

      navigate('/dashboard', {
        replace: true,
      });

      return;
    }

    if (inProgress === InteractionStatus.None) {
      instance.loginRedirect(loginRequest);
    }
  }, [accounts, inProgress, instance, navigate]);

  return (
    <div className="page-canvas flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center px-4">
      <div className="glass-panel w-full max-w-md p-8 text-center">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">
          <span className="text-brand-primary">Win</span>
          <span className="text-accent-orange">Events</span>
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-text-muted">Redirecting to Microsoft login...</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default Login;

FILE: src/shared/components/Modal.tsx

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';

interface ModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  ariaLabel: string;
  className?: string;
  onClose?: () => void;
  children: ReactNode;
}

function Modal({ open, ariaLabel, className = '', onClose, children }: ModalProps) {
  if (!open) {
    return null;
  }

  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[1000] flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-slate-950/10 p-4">
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-label={ariaLabel}
        className={className + ' glass-popup relative'}
      >
        {onClose && (
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label={'Close ' + ariaLabel.toLowerCase() + ' dialog'}
            onClick={onClose}
            className="absolute right-3 top-3 z-10 grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-full text-2xl leading-none text-text-secondary transition hover:bg-surface-subtle hover:text-text-primary focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-primary"
          >
            &times;
          </button>
        )}
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  );
}
interface FeedbackModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  tone?: 'success' | 'error';
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function FeedbackModal({
  open,
  title,
  message,
  tone = 'success',
  onClose,
}: FeedbackModalProps) {
  if (!open) return null;

  const isError = tone === 'error';

  return (
    <Modal
      open={open}
      ariaLabel={title}
      className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-border-accent/40 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl"
      onClose={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className={`mb-4 grid h-11 w-11 place-items-center rounded-full text-xl font-bold ${
          isError ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-700' : 'bg-green-100 text-green-700'
        }`}
        aria-hidden="true"
      >
        {isError ? '!' : '\u2713'}
      </div>
      <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-text-primary">{title}</h2>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-text-secondary">{message}</p>
      <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="rounded-lg bg-brand-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-brand-primary-hover"
        >
          Close
        </button>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

export default Modal;

FILE: src/shared/components/Navigationbar.tsx
================================================================================
import { useMsal } from '@azure/msal-react';
import { Link, NavLink } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useState } from 'react';

import ProfileMenu from './ProfileMenu';
import EventNotifications from './EventNotifications';

function Navigationbar() {
  const { accounts } = useMsal();
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const account = accounts[0];

  // Get the roles assigned to the Microsoft account
  const roles = (account?.idTokenClaims?.roles as string[]) ?? [];

  // Temporary check to see which Microsoft roles are available

  const isAdmin = roles.includes('Admin');
  const isEmployee = roles.includes('Employee');

  const canViewParticipants = isAdmin || isEmployee;

  const canViewAdministration = isAdmin;

  const links = [
    { to: '/dashboard', label: t('navigation.dashboard'), visible: true },
    { to: '/events', label: t('navigation.events'), visible: true },
    {
      to: '/participants',
      label: t('navigation.participants'),
      visible: canViewParticipants,
    },
    { to: '/my-tasks', label: t('navigation.tasks'), visible: isAdmin || isEmployee },
    {
      to: '/administration',
      label: t('navigation.administration'),
      visible: canViewAdministration,
    },
  ].filter((link) => link.visible);

  const renderLinks = () =>
    links.map(({ to, label }) => (
      <NavLink
        key={to}
        to={to}
        onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(false)}
        className={({ isActive }) =>
          `rounded-full px-3 py-2 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors ${
            isActive
              ? 'bg-surface-blue text-brand-primary shadow-sm'
              : 'text-text-secondary hover:bg-surface-subtle hover:text-brand-primary'
          }`
        }
      >
        {label}
      </NavLink>
    ));

  return (
    <nav className="sticky top-0 z-50 border-b border-blue-200/80 bg-white/82 px-4 py-3 shadow-[0_4px_18px_rgba(15,35,55,0.06)] backdrop-blur-xl sm:px-8 sm:py-3">
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-[1500px] items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3 lg:gap-10">
          <Link
            to="/dashboard"
            aria-label={t('navigation.goToDashboard')}
            className="shrink-0 text-[1.7rem] font-extrabold tracking-[-0.06em]"
          >
            <span className="text-blue-500">Win</span>
            <span className="text-orange-500">Events</span>
          </Link>

          <div className="hidden items-center gap-1.5 lg:flex xl:gap-2">{renderLinks()}</div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
          <EventNotifications />
          <ProfileMenu />
          <button
            type="button"
            className="grid h-10 w-10 place-items-center rounded-xl border border-border-default bg-surface-subtle/70 text-xl text-text-secondary transition hover:border-blue-200 hover:bg-surface-blue lg:hidden"
            aria-label={isMenuOpen ? 'Close navigation menu' : 'Open navigation menu'}
            aria-expanded={isMenuOpen}
            aria-controls="mobile-navigation"
            onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen((open) => !open)}
          >
            {isMenuOpen ? '\u00D7' : '\u2630'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        id="mobile-navigation"
        className={`${isMenuOpen ? 'grid' : 'hidden'} gap-4 border-t border-border-default pt-4 lg:hidden`}
      >
        {renderLinks()}
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

export default Navigationbar;

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

interface PageHeaderProps {
  title: string;
  description: ReactNode;
  action?: ReactNode;
  layout?: 'between' | 'responsive' | 'left';
}

function PageHeader({ title, description, action, layout = 'between' }: PageHeaderProps) {
  const layoutClass = {
    between: 'items-center justify-between',
    responsive: 'flex-col justify-between md:flex-row md:items-center',
    left: 'flex-col',
  }[layout];

  return (
    <header className={`page-title-panel mb-6 flex gap-4 ${layoutClass}`}>
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-text-primary">{title}</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-text-muted">{description}</p>
        <span className="page-accent" aria-hidden="true" />
      </div>
      {action}
    </header>
  );
}

export default PageHeader;

FILE: src/shared/components/ProfileMenu.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useMsal } from '@azure/msal-react';

function ProfileMenu() {
  const { instance, accounts } = useMsal();
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const account = instance.getActiveAccount() ?? accounts[0];
  const roles = (account?.idTokenClaims?.roles as string[]) ?? [];

  const role = ['Admin', 'Employee', 'Attendee'].find((item) => roles.includes(item)) ?? 'User';

  const name = account?.name || account?.username || 'User';

  const initials =
    name
      .trim()
      .split(/[\s@.]+/)
      .filter(Boolean)
      .slice(0, 2)
      .map((part) => part[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? '')
      .join('') || 'U';

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleOutsideClick = (event: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setIsOpen(false);
      }
    };

    const handleEscape = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') setIsOpen(false);
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleOutsideClick);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);

    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleOutsideClick);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);
    };
  }, []);

  const handleLogout = () => {
    void instance.logoutRedirect({
      account,
      postLogoutRedirectUri: window.location.origin,
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={menuRef}>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setIsOpen((open) => !open)}
        aria-expanded={isOpen}
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        aria-label="Open profile menu"
        className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-full p-1.5 pr-3 transition hover:bg-gray-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
      >
        <span className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-blue-600 text-sm font-bold text-white ring-2 ring-blue-100">
          {initials}
        </span>

        <span className="hidden min-w-0 text-left sm:block">
          <span className="block max-w-44 truncate text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">
            {name}
          </span>
          <span className="block text-xs text-gray-500">{role}</span>
        </span>

        <svg
          className={`h-4 w-4 text-gray-500 transition-transform ${isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`}
          viewBox="0 0 20 20"
          fill="currentColor"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          <path
            fillRule="evenodd"
            d="M5.23 7.21a.75.75 0 011.06.02L10 11.168l3.71-3.938a.75.75 0 111.09 1.032l-4.25 4.51a.75.75 0 01-1.09 0l-4.25-4.51a.75.75 0 01.02-1.052z"
            clipRule="evenodd"
          />
        </svg>
      </button>

      {isOpen && (
        <div
          role="menu"
          className="glass-popup absolute right-0 z-50 mt-3 w-64 overflow-hidden"
        >
          <div className="border-b border-gray-100 px-4 py-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <span className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-blue-600 font-bold text-white">
                {initials}
              </span>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900">{name}</p>
                <p className="truncate text-xs text-gray-500">{account?.username}</p>
                <span className="mt-1 inline-flex rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700">
                  {role}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            role="menuitem"
            onClick={handleLogout}
            className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-left text-sm font-medium text-red-600 transition hover:bg-red-50"
          >
            <svg
              className="h-5 w-5"
              viewBox="0 0 24 24"
              fill="none"
              stroke="currentColor"
              strokeWidth="1.8"
              aria-hidden="true"
            >
              <path
                strokeLinecap="round"
                strokeLinejoin="round"
                d="M15 3h4a2 2 0 012 2v14a2 2 0 01-2 2h-4M10 17l5-5-5-5m5 5H3"
              />
            </svg>
            Sign out
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default ProfileMenu;

interface SearchBarProps {
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  placeholder?: string;
  fullWidth?: boolean;
}

function SearchBar({ value, onChange, placeholder = 'Search...', fullWidth = false }: SearchBarProps) {
  return (
    <input
      type="text"
      placeholder={placeholder}
      value={value}
      onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
      className={`w-full rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-text-primary outline-none transition placeholder:text-text-placeholder focus:border-brand-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-primary/20 ${fullWidth ? '' : 'md:max-w-xs'}`}
    />
  );
}

export default SearchBar;

================================================================================
FILE: src/shared/components/StatusBadge.tsx
================================================================================
interface StatusBadgeProps {
  status: string;
  context?: 'event' | 'task';
}

function StatusBadge({ status, context = 'event' }: StatusBadgeProps) {
  const normalizedStatus = status.trim().toLowerCase();
  let colorClass = 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 text-gray-700';

  if (context === 'event') {
    if (normalizedStatus === 'scheduled') {
      colorClass = 'border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700';
    } else if (normalizedStatus === 'cancelled' || normalizedStatus === 'canceled') {
      colorClass = 'border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-700';
    } else if (normalizedStatus === 'upcoming') {
      colorClass = 'border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700';
    } else if (normalizedStatus === 'ongoing') {
      colorClass = 'border-green-200 bg-green-50 text-green-700';
    } else if (normalizedStatus === 'past' || normalizedStatus === 'completed') {
      colorClass = 'border-violet-200 bg-violet-50 text-violet-700';
    }
  } else if (normalizedStatus === 'completed') {
    colorClass = 'border-green-200 bg-green-50 text-green-700';
  } else if (normalizedStatus === 'inprogress' || normalizedStatus === 'in progress') {
    colorClass = 'border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700';
  } else if (normalizedStatus === 'pending') {
    colorClass = 'border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 text-yellow-700';
  } else if (normalizedStatus === 'overdue') {
    colorClass = 'border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-700';
  }

  return (
    <span className={`inline-flex rounded-lg border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium ${colorClass}`}>
      {status}
    </span>
  );
}

export default StatusBadge;


================================================================================
FILE: src/shared/hooks/useAccessToken.ts
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useMsal } from '@azure/msal-react';

const API_SCOPE = 'api://0332cc25-1dc3-4542-b1cd-a1ad23d0f620/access_as_user';
const DEFAULT_MISSING_ACCOUNT_MESSAGE = 'No Microsoft account is logged in.';

export function useAccessToken(missingAccountMessage = DEFAULT_MISSING_ACCOUNT_MESSAGE) {
  const { instance, accounts } = useMsal();
  const account = instance.getActiveAccount() ?? accounts[0];
  const accountRef = useRef(account);
  useEffect(() => {
    accountRef.current = account;
  }, [account]);

  const getAccessToken = useCallback(async () => {
    const activeAccount = instance.getActiveAccount() ?? accountRef.current;
    if (!activeAccount) {
      throw new Error(missingAccountMessage);
    }

    const response = await instance.acquireTokenSilent({
      scopes: [API_SCOPE],
      account: activeAccount,
    });

    return response.accessToken;
  }, [instance, missingAccountMessage]);

  return { account, getAccessToken };
}
